# Book of the Mysteries of Heaven and Earth II

**Author**: Bahayla Mikael  
**Series**: PO Tome 6  
**Columns**: pp. 383–458

ነገር ፡ ወዜና ፡ ሕይወት ፤ ትርጓሜ ፡ ራእዩ ፣፡ ለዮሐንስ ፡ አጻብኢሁ ፡ አለ ፡ ጸሐፉ ፣ አስሩ ፡ ወንጌለ ፡ መለ”ኮት ፣፡ መርሐ ፡ ይኩናኒ' ፣ ለመንገለ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ወኀበ ፡ ሖርኩ ፡ ፍና። በስመ ፡ ሥሉስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡እትናገር ፣ ወእስእል ፡ ምሥጢሮ ፡ለእግዚአብሔር። ይቤ ፡ አብ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ አባ ፡ ዞሲማስ ፣ ዘውእቱ ፡ በኀይለ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ይቤ ፡ ቆምኩ ፡ ዓመ ዓልተ ፣፤ ወጓሌሊተ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቀላየ ፡ ማይ ፡ በከመ ፡ ጾመ ፣ ሙሴ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ጾመ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ወነበበ ፡ ወንጌለ ፡ ቀዳሚሁ ፡ ቃል ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ እስከ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ይብ ል ፡ወዘሂ ፡ኮነ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ሕይወት ፣ ውእቱ ። ወካልእ ፣ መልአክ ፡ነበበ፣ ወይቤ ፡ ወሀሎ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ብእሲ ፣ ዘተፈነወ ፣ እምኅበ ፣ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘስሙ ፣ ዮሐ
THE LAST THREE TREATISES OF THE BOOK OF THE MYSTERIES OF HEAVEN AND EARTH II. - INTERPRETATION OF THE APOCALYPSE (SECOND TREATISE) 1. Title of the treatise. - * Exposition, living explanation and interpretation of the Apocalypse of John. May his ten fingers, which wrote the Gospel of the * divinity, be for me a guide toward the truth and a direction (for) where I am going! In the name of the holy Trinity, I expose and I invoke the mystery of the Lord. The blessed Father Abba Zosimâs, that is to say Bahayela Mik'âêl, said: I stood forty days and forty nights in a chasm of water. As Moses had fasted, John also then fasted much. He stated the Gospel, saying: In the beginning was the Word, up to (the place) where he said: This took place through Him. In Him was life. Another angel spoke and said: There was a man, who was sent by the Lord, called John.

nights. Every Friday, he took bands and waited * for the day of his death. As the Lord had told him, on the mountain he found one day two angels in the process of digging a tomb. One (was) Gabriel and the other Michael. Moses said to them: May the Lord help you! They said to him: Be blessed! Come, measure the tomb for us! When he had measured (it), they buried him and they left him there. They cried out and they said: We have buried the man of the Lord. When they said that, they were standing in the midst of the people. The people ran and climbed the mountain. They could not (find) the tomb of Moses.

3. Names of the Angels of the seven Churches of Asia. - Here are what were the words (of John), after I had spoken. John the Virgin came, showed me the book of science and began to explain to me the interpretation of the book of his vision. He spoke to me of the seven Churches * when he said that the apostles had erected seven Churches in their first preaching. The name of the angel of the Church of Ephesus is Damâtrê. It is he who holds firm seven stars in his right (hand), that is to say seven angels. The seven golden luminaries are the seven Churches. The name of the angel of Smyrna (Sardinon) is Taboden. It is therefore the seat of Peter. He who was thrown into prison who (is it), I say? || He said to me: It is Peter, my brother.

(The apostles) had to suffer for ten days that is to say the ten days (during) which the apostles fasted, after (Christ) had ascended (to heaven). The angel of Pergamum (Parâgmon), what is his name? The apostle spoke to me and said to me: His name is 'lyâsou. The angel of Thyatira (Tiyâtron), what is his name? He said to me: * His name is Digéba'êl. I say again: The angel of Sardis (Sardisson), what is his name? The apostle said to me: His name is Foubêfên. I say: The angel of Philadelphia (Feldelfyä), what is his name? The apostle said to me: His name is Rehë'êl. It is therefore Simon II. The door is open and no one can close it means baptism. Furthermore, I say: The angel of Laodicea (Ladouqyân), what is his name? The Theologian said to me: His name is Gabriel. He said to me: Understand and hear the mystery that I will expose to you; hear, listener. He says: The seven (Churches) are those that were named formerly by the apostles.

4. The book with seven seals. - Word of John Abou Qalamsis. He saw a book with seven seals. The book * is Adam. The seven seals are those that (the Lord) gave to Adam: royalty, priesthood, prophecy, principality, domination, faith, purity. He said: Open the seals, O Lord, for human creatures. All the various (seals) are for men. You see that clearly he said to you: I was killed and I redeemed (man)

by my blood. What did (Christ) redeem by his blood, if not Adam? Regarding the seven seals the interpreter spoke. 5. The Lamb with seven horns and seven eyes. - The Lamb with seven horns and seven eyes means the Son. The horns are the patriarchs, the archbishops, the bishops, the priests, the deacons, the readers. The seven eyes mean the seven books of the Churches. The first is * the Gospel in its entirety. The second is the Epistles, which the Apostles sent. The third is the Acts of the Apostles. The fourth is the Epistles of Paul in their entirety. The fifth is the Sinodos in its entirety. The sixth is (the book) of Abou Qalamsis, (the book) of the Apocalypse. The seventh is the book of the Kidân and the Didascalia in a single volume.

6. Explanation of the seven seals. - Here is what the angel of the Lord expressed and interpreted. The first seal means the first epoch of the world. A white horse went out that is to say Adam. He who rides on him is Christ, who put on the flesh of man. He holds firm the bow that is to say the staff of the cross of the Unique, as Job said: For me, I go, while my staff is in my hands. Furthermore, (the Lord) * said thus in the Gospel: They took him, they made him go outside, they led him, while they carried his cross that is to say they gave him a crown, and he went out, in order to conquer. That means that He, endowed with a mortal flesh, has

conquered Satan. A second horse went out that is to say Noah; (a horse) red that is to say the blood and the slaughter, which occurred in the days of Noah.

At the third seal, a black horse went out that is to say the worship of idols. A measure of wheat (will be worth) a denarius. The denarius means faith. The wheat is the flesh of Our Lord. One will not exhaust the wine and the oil. The wine means the blood of Our Lord. The oil is baptism. These are these (substances), which * will not have any lack until the end of the world. At the fourth seal, a green horse went out that is to say the Holy Law. The name of him who rides on him is Death. That means that the Law said: Let him who has killed be killed. But the Gospel says: To him who slapped you (on) one cheek, offer him the other also. What shall we say of this word? The Gospel is the life and the food of the spirit. Indeed, the food of the body is bread. But the food of the spirit is the word of the Scriptures. At the fifth seal, what John saw under the altar, was the soul of those who were killed because of the word of the Lord. These are the prophets who were killed at the fifth * epoch.
ንቱ ፡ እለ ፡ ተቀትሉ ፡ በኃምስ ፡ ዘ”መን ። ወበሳድስ ፡ ማሕተም ፡ ኮነ ፡ ድልቅልቅ ፡ ዘይቤሂ ፡ ዘመነ ፡ ስቅለቱ ፣ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ውእቱ ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ አድለቅለቀት ፡ ምድር ፡ ወተሰጥቀ ፡ ኩከ።ኩሕ ፡ ወው
At the sixth seal, an earthquake occurred that is to say the epoch of the crucifixion of Our Lord, as the Gospel says: The earth shook; the rock split; the blood flowed and entered the mouth of Adam

The sun darkened that is to say - the Gospel tells you - the sun darkened from the sixth hour until the ninth hour. The stars fell from heaven that is to say the people of Israel. When he had opened the seventh seal, everything that was in heaven and what was on earth was silent for half an hour. Was silent, that is to say the end of the world, as Ezra says: The world will be silent on the seventh day, meaning by that that the world will be consumed and that * silence will occur on the seventh day.

7. The trumpet blasts of the angels. - Word of the mystery of Abou Qalamsis. When the first angel had sounded the trumpet, there occurred hail and fire, mixed with blood, (which) descended on the earth. This is about Adam. The second angel, who sounded the trumpet, means the second plague: the hail and the fire, which descended on the earth a (true) deluge of hail. When the third angel had sounded the trumpet, a great star descended from heaven, burning like fire it was Satan. As to how Satan was called a star, listen to Isaiah, when he says: How has the star fallen, which rose from the side of the Orient. It descended on the third part of the rivers. The rivers mean the prophets. The springs of water are the children * of the prophets. As for the wormwood it is Satan. Indeed,


[p. 390] {#po.6.p390}
ቢያት ፡፡ ወእጐስታርሂ ፡ ሰይጣን ፣ ውእቱ ። እስመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ትምህርቱ ፡ ለሰይጣን ፣ መሪር ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘደኃሪቱ ፡ ይወስድ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ሀጐል ፡፡
all the doctrine of Satan is bitter, (doctrine) whose end leads to perdition.
ቃል ፡ ወነገር ፡ ወምሥጢር ፡ዘአቡ ፡ ቀለምሲስ ፡፡ ይቤ ፡ ወበጥቅዐተ ፡ ራብፅ ፡ መል አክ ፣ ተቀስፈት ፡ ፀሐይ ፣ ወጸልመት ፡፣ ሣልስተ ፡ እዴሃ ፤ ወሳልስተ ፡ እዴሆሙ ፡ ለወ ርኅ ፡ ወለዕለት ፣ ወለሌሊት ።፡ ይቤ ፡ መተርጉም ፡ ዘፈነዎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡=፡ ተቀስ ፈት ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ በእንተ ፣ እብራዊያን ፡ እምኅበ ፡ ፈርዖን ።፡ እስመ ፡ በፀሐይ ፡ አስተማሰሎሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ እምነቶሙ ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ እምነተ ፡ አበዊሆሙ ፡ ጽንዕት ፡ ወጸልመት ፡ ሣልስት ፡ እዴሃ ፡ ዘይቤሂ ፡ "እስመ ፡ ቦ፳ሕፃናት ፡ ሞቱ ፡ በውስተ ፡ እ ስራኤል ፡ በ፳ዕለት ፡ አመ ፣ በከየት ፡ ራሔል ። ወራሔልሰ ፡ ብእሲተ ፡ ካህን ፡ ይእ ቲ፡ ወዕበ ፡ ዓፀቦሙ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ግንፋል ፣ በአድኅጾ ፣ መንታ ፣ ውሉድ' ፡ወገረት ፡ ውስ ተ ፡ ጸብረ ፡ ግንፋል ። ወጸርሐት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትብል ፣ ሀሎኑ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ።፡
Word, exposition and mystery of Abou Qalamsis. He said: At the sounding (of the trumpet) of the fourth angel, the sun was struck; its third part darkened, as well as the third part of the moon, of the day and of the night. The interpreter, whom the Lord sent, said: The sun was struck that is to say the Hebrews (were struck) by Pharaoh; indeed, he had compared them to the sun, because of their belief and because of the strong belief of their fathers. The third part (of the sun) darkened * that is to say 30,000 children died in Israel in one day, when Rachel wept. Rachel was the wife of a priest. When the work of the bricks was hard for (the Israelites, Rachel), by aborting two twin sons, threw (them) into the clay of the bricks and cried, saying: Is God in heaven? Then the Lord descended on Mount Sinai. The Lord spoke to Moses and said to him: Certainly I have heard the cry of my people and I have descended, in order to save it

He who interpreted, he who spoke through the mouth of the Holy Spirit said: The fifth seal is the (fifth) epoch. The star is John the Baptist, to whom the key of baptism was given. The smoke, * which rose from the chasm, is the Spirit. The sun is the Only Begotten Son, whom the Holy Spirit covered then by a kind of cloud. The Gospel tells you clearly: A luminous cloud came and overshadowed them. The locusts, which came out of the chasm, (to come) on earth, are the peoples, who received the power to fulfill the priestly functions from the king of heaven and earth. It was ordered (to the locusts) not to destroy the grass. The grass means the prophecy of the prophets; it is (the prophecy), which was called grass, as Solomon says: Gather toward you the grass of the mountain. The green (plants) mean the children of baptism. Those who do not have the seal of the Creator * on their forehead are the impious and the demons. The five months mean the

impious, who will be tortured in the furnace from the days of the false Messiah. Indeed, water will disappear from the face of the earth. Then a great suffering will occur on the earth. That is why (the Scripture) tells you clearly: In those days, man will seek death and will not find it. 8. The mystery of John Abou Qalamsis. - Word of the mystery of John Abou Qalamsis. Such is the vision of the locusts. They resemble horses, ready for war. These are the Christians, who fight with Satan, as Paul says: Indeed, your fight is not with flesh and blood, * but with the impure spirits, who dominate the world of darkness that is to say the demons. No one will overcome them, except the one who has humility, faith and charity. When Satan sees the humility of men, he will burn and become like a flame.

As to how this exposition here is true, listen to the Gospel (and) what Our Lord said: Two men went up to the temple, in order to pray. The humble one returned, being justified, and the proud one, being condemned. They have as king the angel of the abyss. The abyss means the Church. The king is the Son. His name in Hebrew is Abdon and in Greek Apolyon. That means that * the Hebrews looked at him (as) crazy. Apolyon means the wise of the elect. In the days of the

9. The hidden Scriptures. - Concerning the Scriptures, which are hidden. Of the Law (there are) three, as Ezra says: (The Lord) said to Moses: Expose this and hide this; of David five, as (he himself) says; of Isaiah seven, as he himself says: The rest of my exposition exists; of Zechariah one, as he himself says: I saw a sickle, (which) was leaping in the sky. The sickle * is the Word of the Lord, which will harvest the impious for punishment and the elect for life; of Jeremiah three, as the Lord says: Hide my own exposition. Furthermore, he says: I will take one (man) from the city and two from the province; I will lead them into Zion and establish them as judges. Here is the explanation. One from the city means the Israelites, who were present at the preaching of the apostles. Two from the province means the descendants of Ham and the descendants of Japheth, who were present at the preaching of the apostles; of Sirach two, as he himself says: Until you find the time (to reveal it), hide your exposition.
ወበእንተ ፣ ነጐድ"ጓድ ፡ ሕትም ፡ ዘይቤ ፣፤ ጸሐብታት ፡፣ አለ ፡ ተሐብኡ ፡ እምሰብ
10. The seven hidden gifts. - Concerning the seven thunders * seal (your mouth). They mean the seven gifts, which have been hidden from men.

There are good ones among them, but there are bad ones. The first is death, which has been hidden in the heart of man, as Paul says: Behold: I will expose to you a hidden exposition: it is that we shall all die. The second is faith, which has been hidden in the heart of the impious, as Paul himself says: Our teaching is hidden from the impious. The third is charity; indeed, while charity is good, man seeks hatred and quarreling. The fourth is the fear of the Lord, which is forgotten by the heart of man. The fifth (is water), since water is the essence of man. This is wonderful. The sixth is milk, (which) comes out * of a breast. When it has been sliced, blood will occur. When a cow has calved, the calf flees to its mother's breast. For man, it is the same. The seventh is the mercy of the Lord, which is hidden in the heart of man, as he himself says: The mystery of my mercy will not be divulged to any (being, endowed) with a body.

11. Incredulity at the exposition of the mystery. - Behold, I have finished for you the exposition of the mysteries. But it seems to me that, when I have made this exposition to the children of men, they will contradict you, saying: Nothing of the Scriptures is hidden. With such (beings) you will not argue, but on the contrary, you will seek suffering for yourself. Listen to what Our Lord said in the book of Mysteries. Now, the book * of Mysteries is


[p. 395] {#po.6.p395}
ርሰ ፡ዘውእቱ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ኪዳን ። ይቤ ፡ አለ ፡ ይዘርኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ፡ ዘኢይበ ቅዕ ፤ ሕማመ ፡፣ የኅሥሱ ፡ ለርአሶሙ ። ወጳውሎስኒ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ወመስተካሕድ ፡ ብእ ሲ ፡ አምከመ ፡ ገሠጽኮ ፡ ምዕረ ፡ ወካዕበ ፡ ሕድጎ ፡ ወአእምሮ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዓላዊ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወነገረ ፡ ጥበብሰ ፡ ይጌይስ ፡ ትንግር' ፡ ለጠቢባን ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፣ ጳውሎስ ፡፣ጥበበ፡፣
the book of the Kidân. He said: Those who sow in a land that is not fruitful, seek suffering for themselves. Paul also said: Man is a slinger, every time you punish him. And again: Leave him and know that he is a pervert. As for the exposition of wisdom, it is better that you expose it to the wise, as Paul says: We will expose wisdom to the wise.

12. Origin of the prophets. - Listen also to this exposition. The prophets, on one hand, the apostles, on the other, came out of Jacob. The house of Israel was called (a flock of) sheep, as Enoch says: From the wild ass was born the ox, that is to say Abraham from Terah. The goat was born * from the goat, that is to say Jacob from Isaac. As for Esau, he called him a wild boar. The prophet Enoch named the various peoples: the Amalekites: eagles; the Egyptians: hyenas; the Ishmaelites: wild asses; the Arabs: goats; the Canaanites: ravens; the Philistines: foxes; the Moabites: mice; the Persians: lions; the Ammonites: scorpions; the Midianites: serpents; the descendants of Japheth: leopards; the Edomites: wolves. The prophet Enoch named all the various (peoples), (he) who wrote the exposition (of events) to come, before they happened. I am now going to speak again of the prophets. The Apostle said: The first of all * the prophets is Adam, our father. His mother is the earth. His father is the Lord,


[p. 396] {#po.6.p396}
ሙ ፡ በሬካ ፳ ወኖኅኒ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ወልዱ ፣ ለማቱሰላ ፣ አቡሁ ፣ ለላሜኅ ፡ ወእሙ ፡ ቤቴ ኔስ =፡ ውእቱኒ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘተነበየ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ አይሕ ፡ ውስተ ፤ ዓለም ፡=፡
his Creator. As for Enoch, his father is Jared and his mother is Baraka. Noah is the son of the son of Methuselah, father of Lamech. His mother is Betenes. He is the prophet, who prophesied (and) exposed that the flood would come into the world.

13. Abraham. - Abraham is also a prophet, who prophesied much. The Book of Jubilees tells you so. Abraham prophesied, saying: Children will rise to renew your law. These are the apostles. Furthermore, he told you: The world will be (in) peace. This is concerning the Son, for he put the world (in) peace, as Paul says: He made peace by his cross. When Abraham had accomplished the sacrifice, * the Lord said to him: Take a three-year-old ox, a three-year-old goat, a dove and a turtledove. Here is the explanation. Abraham is explained by the Son. The three-year-old ox means Adam. The three years are the three epochs. Of (three) years means that the peoples came out of the three sons of Noah. With them three Our Lord took the peoples by the preaching of the Gospel. The dove is Mary Our Lady. The turtledove is the apostle Paul. The birds, do not cut them. The birds mean the doctors. They will not be interrupted until the end of the world. * Towards evening means near the end of the world. Our Lord also said: The moment of the approach of evening is coming, (that is to say the moment) near the end of the


[p. 397] {#po.6.p397}
ቤ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ በሕልቀተ ፡ ዓለም ።፡ መጽአ' ፡ ወልድ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስዓራ ፡ ለኅጢአት ፣ ወነድሂ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወዕቶንሂ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ውእቱ ።፡
world, as Paul says: At the end of the world, the Son will come, in order to annihilate sin. The flame is the Son. The furnace is the cross.

14. The names of the fathers and mothers of the prophets. - Abba Bahayela Mika'el said: "Expose to me then again how the prophets came forth. Expose to me the names of their fathers and their mothers, he said again." The explainer said: "Abraham came from Terah, for he is a descendant of Shem. The name of his mother is 'Ewasa, that is to say Tehdif. From Abraham the nations and the peoples diversified. Truly * the posterity of Jacob teemed with prophets. The first prophet in the Law is Moses, (descendant) of Melkam. His brother is the priest Aaron. They are of the house of Levi. The name of their father is 'Enbaram. The name of their mother is Yokabed. The name of their sister is Mary. Joshua is the son of Newi, it is said. Samuel, of the house of Levi, is (descendant) of Mirari. His father is Helgana. The name of his mother is Hanna. She is of the house of Israel. David is the prophet and the king, whose origin is from the tribe of Judah. His father is Jesse. The name of his mother is Hebli'ale. From (the time) when he prophesied until the coming of the Son, (there are) 1800 years. Solomon, his * son, is a prophet. His mother is Bersabeh. The prophet Elijah is of the tribe of Levi, descendant of Phinehas, son of the priest 'Al'azar. The name of his father is 'Iyasenyou. The name of his mother is Bitona. Isaiah is of the tribe of Simeon. His

mother is of the house of Reuben. The name of his father is Amos. The name (of his mother) is 'Arenes. From (the time) when he prophesied until the coming of the Beloved, (there are) 765 years. Jeremiah is of the tribe of Levi, who (himself descends) from Qa'at. The name of his father is Kelqyou. The name of his mother is Weeteles. His dwelling was at 'Anatot, land of Benjamin. Daniel is of the tribe of Judah. The name of his father is 'Iyo'agem, king of Judah. His mother is Melenes. From (the time) when he prophesied until the coming of the Word, (there are) 404 years. As for the three children, the name of their father is Semyou. The name of their mother is Natri, and also Hanna. The father of Daniel and the father of the three children were brothers. Ezekiel is of the tribe of Levi, (descendant) of Qa'at. The name of his father is Bouz. The name of his mother is Nore. From (the time) when he prophesied until the coming of the Unique, (there are) 464 years. Ezra, who is called Soutou'el, is of the tribe of Levi. The name of his father is Seryou. The name of his mother is Salo. From (the time) when he prophesied until the coming * of the Son, (there are) 537 (years). Tobit is of the house of Naphtali. The name of his father is Geba'el. The name of his mother is Mesede. 'Iyasou Sirak is the son of the priest 'Al'azar, that is to say (of the) priest Simeon. His

name is Senna. Hosea is of the tribe of Joseph. The name of his father is Be'erem. The name of his mother is Mesele. Amos is of the tribe of Simeon. The name of his father is Zitar. The name of his mother is Misata. It is therefore he who is the father of the prophet Isaiah.

Micah is of the tribe of Benjamin. The name of his father is 'Amda, that is to say Mourat. The name of his mother is Satamo, that is to say Sitani. Joel is of the tribe of Reuben. The name of his father is Reuben. The name of his mother is Misiri, * that is to say Batou'el. Obadiah is of the tribe of Ephraim. The name of his father is Kokia. The name of his mother is Safte. Jonah is of the house of Issachar. The name of his father is 'Amate. The name of his mother is Matale. Nahum is of the tribe of Zebulun. The name of his father is Helqsyous. The name of his mother is 'Anbina, that is to say Sona. Habakkuk is of the tribe of Simeon. The name of his father is Dakor. The name of his mother is Sourafa'a, that is to say Rasfya, for she was begotten on the (happy) news of a Seraph. Zephaniah is of the tribe of Naphtali. The name of his father is Sedeq, that is to say Gotolyal the Amorite. The name of his mother is Meroba. He is also called Neheb. The Amorite means that (the Israelites) inherited the land * of Amoryon.
ርዮን ። ወሐንኒ ፡ እምነገደ ፡ ጋድ ፡ ወእሙኒ ፡፣ እምነገደ ፡ ዳን ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ወስመ ፣ አቡሁ ፤

አጦታ ፡ ወስመ ፡ እሙ ፡ ሌኔ ። ወዘካርያስኒ ፡ እምነገደ ፣ ሌዊ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወስመ ፡ አ ቡሁ፡ በራኪዩ ፡ ወስመ ፡ እመ ፡ እሙ ፡ ሐዶ ፡ ወስመ ፡ እሙ፤ ፡ ኅሌ ። ወምልክያስኒ ፡ እምነገደ ፡ አሴር ፣ ውእቱ ፡ ወስመ ፡ አቡሁ ፣ ፋሔል ፡ ወስመ ፡ እሙ ፡ ጽጌኔ ። ወጋድ ኒ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ እምነገደ ፡ ዳን ፤ ውእቱ ፡ ወስመ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ሞጽ ። ወስመ ፡ እሙ ፡ነቢ፣ እንተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ነቢል ። ወዮሐንስኒ ፡ መክብበ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ እምቤተ ፡ ሌዊ ፡ ውእቱ ወ ስመ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ዘክርያስ ፡ ወስመ ፡ አሙ ፡ ኤልሳቤጥ ። ወለሐጌኒ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ስመ ፡ አሙ፣ ሶና ። እንተ ፣ ይእቲ ፡ ሌኒ ። ወከመዝ ፣ ጸአቶ”ሙ ፡ ለነቢያት ፡ እም፲ወ፪ደቂቀ ፡ ያዕ ቆብ ፡ ወአልቦ ፣ ነቢይ ፣ ዘኢወፅአ ፡ እምያዕቆብ ፡ እንበለ ፡ ኢዮብ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፣ ዘተ
of Levi. The name of his father is Barakiyou. The name of his mother's mother is Hado. The name of his mother is Hale. Malachi is of the tribe of Asher. The name of his father is Fahel. The name of his mother is Tsegene. The prophet Gad is of the tribe of Dan. The name of his father is Mots. The name of his mother is Nabi, that is to say Nabil. John, who closes the choir of the prophets, is of the house of Levi. The name of his father is Zechariah. The name of his mother is Elizabeth. As for Haggai again, the name of his mother is Sona, that is to say Leni. Such is the origin * of the prophets: (they come) from the twelve sons of Jacob. There is no prophet who did not come out of Jacob, except Job alone, who is likened to the Son. Now, Job is of the house of Esau.
መሰለ ፡ በወልድ ።፡ ወኢዮብኒ ፡ እምቤተ ፡ ዔሳው ፡ ውእቱ ፡=፡ ወሐዋርያሂኒ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘኢወፅአ ፡ እምያዕቆብ ፡= ወበእንተ ፣ ሐዋርያኒ ፡ ተነበየ ፡ ሕዝቅኤል ፡= ወበእንተ ፣ ወልድኒ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እንተ ፡ ፕኅት ፡ ቦአ ፡ ወወፅአ ፣ እን ዘ ፣ ሀለወት ፡ እፁታ ፡፡ ፕኅትሰ ፣ ማርያም ። ። ድንግል ፡ ይእቲ ፡፡ ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤ ፣ ርኢኩ ፡ ፄሰብአ ፡ ድብ ፡ እለ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ እምደቡብ ፡፡ ወኒአብያጺሖሙ ፡ እለ ፡ መጽ ኡ ፡ እምጽባሕ ፡ ወዙሎሙ ፡ ኾጐድብ ፡ ውስተ ፣፤ አደዊሆሙ ።፡፡ ወእሉሰ ፡ ፲ወ፪ሐዋርያ ት ፡ ትርጓሜሆሙ ። ወኾጐድብኒ ፡ ቃለ ፡ እግዚአብ” ሔር ፡ ውእቱ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ በወ ንጌል ፡ ጐድብ ፡ ተሰይመ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ጉንደ ፡ ዕፀው ፡=፡ ወዕፀው ፡ ዘይቤ ፣ ሰብእ ፡ እሙ
15. The apostles. - As for the apostles, there are none who did not come out of Jacob. Concerning the apostles Ezekiel prophesied. Concerning the Son also he said: He entered and exited through the gate, while it was closed. The gate is the Virgin Mary. He said again: I saw seven men (with) an axe, who were coming from the north, and seven (of) their companions, who were coming from the East. All (had) an axe in their hand. This is the explanation of the twelve apostles. The axe is the word * of the Lord, as (the Lord) says in the Gospel: The axe is laid to the trunk of the trees. The trees mean men.


[p. 401] {#po.6.p401}
ንቱ ፡=፡ ወበእንተዝ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ሕዝቅኤል ፣፡ ወኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ አልቦሙ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ ግዝ ሙ ፡ ወአለሰ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ ሕድጉ ።= ወዝኒ ፡ ብሒል ፡ አለ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ አሚነ፣ ሥላሴ ፡ ይድኅኑ ፡ ወአለ ፡ አልቦሙ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡፣ ዘይቤሂ ፡ እለ ፡ አልቦሙ ፡ አሚነ፣ ይትገዝዘሙ ፡ እምሕረቱ ።፡ ወእምነ ፡ ቅዱላንየ ፡ ቅድሙ ፡ ዘይቤሂ ፣ እስራኤል ፡ እሙን ቱ ፡ ለአሉኒ ፡ ለእመ ፡ አበዩ ፡ አሚነ ፡ ግዝሙ ፡ ብሂል ፡፡
That is why Ezekiel said: Cut off all those who do not have the sign. As for those who have the sign, leave (them). This also means that those who have faith (in) the Trinity will be saved, but that those who do not have the sign, that is to say those who do not have the faith, will be cut off from the mercy of the (Lord). Begin with my saints means (with) Israel. This means: If they themselves also reject the faith, cut (them) off.

16. Exegesis of some biblical texts. - Zechariah said concerning the Son: I saw in the night a man, who was riding on a red horse. He was standing between two mountains, which had shade. * Behind him, there were red, black, multicolored, and white horses. The night is the name of this world before the coming of the Son, as Isaiah says: In the night, my spirit surges toward you. The horse is Adam, who clothed himself with Christ. The man means Christ, who clothed himself (with the flesh of) Our Lady Mary. He was standing between two mountains means between the New and the Old (Testament). Behind him, there were red horses means the apostles, who became red by the blood of their martyrdom; black means the heretics, who were in the days of the apostles and those who came (afterwards), whom one calls Arius and Nestorius; * multicolored means the Christians whose faith was not


[p. 402] {#po.6.p402}
ጽሩየ ፡ ሃይማኖቶሙ ፡ አለ ፡ ይብልዎሙ ፡ ልዮን ፡ ንስጥሮስ" ፡፡ አውጣኪዮስ ፣ ወመቅ ዶንዮስ ፡ ወሰባልዮስ ፣ ወባእዳንሂ ፡ እለ ፡ ከማሆሙ ፡ ብዙኃን ። ወካዕበ ፡ አፍራስ ፡ጸ ዓድው ፡ ይቤ ፤ ፻፻፲ወቿርቱዓነ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ጸዓዳ ፡ ወብሩህ ፣ ሃይማኖቶሙ ።፡፡ ወት ምሀርቶሙኒ ፡ ጽዱል ፡ ከመ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡=፡
pure, those whom one calls Leo, Nestorius, Eutyches, Macedonius, Sabellius and many others still, who are like them; white horses, furthermore, means the 318 orthodox, whose faith was white and luminous, and whose doctrine was brilliant like the sun.
ወዮሐንስኒ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዜነወኒ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ዘመጽአ ፡ ቀዲሙ ፡፡ ወይቤ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ጠ ቅዓ ፡ ሣድስ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ መንፈቀ ፡ ሣድስ ፡ ዘመን ፡ ተነበዩ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ ወ ዘርኡ ፣ ወባዕዳን ፡ አረሩ ፣ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ኢሳይያስ ፡ ዘዘርአ ፣ ሥድሰ ፡ ያአቱ ፡ ስል ሰ ፡ ወዘአረሩኒ ፡ ሥልሰ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ ውእቶሙ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ስ”ላሴ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ አረሩ ፡ ዘዘርኡ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ ወሥድሰ ፡ ዘርኡ ፣፡ ዘይቤሂ ፡ ፳ኦሪተ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ወሣድስ ፡መ ጽሐፈ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ ኩሎሙ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ካልእ ፡ዘይዘርእ ፡፡ ወካልፅ ፣ ዘየአርር ። ወኢሳይያስኒ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እስመ ፣ ኢያሰልጥ ፣ ጽምድ ፡፣ ብእ ራ ፡ ወኢይገብር ፡ አሐደ ፡ ፀሕበ ፡=፡ ዝኒ ፡ ብሂል ፡ ኦሪት ፡ ወነቢያት ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘአስለ ጡ ፡ ወኢይገብር ፡ አሐደ ፡ ፃሕብ ፡ ዘይቤሂ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ መጥምቅ ፡ ዘኢተሰጐረ ፡ ገቦ ሁ ፡ ለአድኅኖ ፡ አዳም ፡ ወውሉዱ ። ወጸሕብ ፣ ብሂል ፡ ስፉረት ፣ ብሂል ። ወከመሰ ፣ ስረት ፡ ብሂል ፤ ውእቱ ፡ ፃሕብ ፡ ስምዖ ፡ ለሲራክ ፡፣ እንዘ ፣ ይብል ፡ ልቡ ፣ ለአብ
John said: The angel, who came in the first place, announced to me and said: When the sixth angel has sounded the trumpet and when half of the sixth epoch has occurred, the prophets will prophesy and sow, but others will harvest, as Isaiah says: He who has sown 6 (measures) will harvest 3 (measures). Those who harvested 3 (measures) are the apostles, the men * of the Trinity, for they harvested what the prophets sowed. (The prophets) sowed 6 (measures) means the 5 (books of) the Law of Moses, the sixth being the book of all the prophets. That is why Our Lord said: Other (is) he who sows and other (is) he who harvests. Isaiah also said: Indeed, (the work of) a pair of oxen will not fill and will not make a pot. This also means the Law and the Prophets. Will not fill and will not make a pot means John the Baptist, whose side was not pierced, to save Adam and his children. The pot means the hole. As to how the pot means the hole, listen to Sirach, when he says: The intelligence of the fool is like a holed pot.
ድ ፡ ከመ ፡ የሕብ ፡ ስቁር ፡፡ ወበሣድስ ፡ ዘመን ፡ ተሰምዓ ፡ ቃል ፡ 8እም”ነ ፡ አቅርንተ ፡ ምሥዋዕ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ቃ

word, which was said means the Unique Son, the Word of the Father and the Word of the Holy Spirit. The horns are the four patriarchs of Ephesus, Antioch, Alexandria and Rome, the great seat of Peter. The sixth angel means the Son. The four angels mean the four Evangelists. The river Euphrates means the holy Church. The horses mean the thousands of Christians. The helmet of fire means the faith in the Father, the Son and the Holy Spirit. The heads of their horses are like the heads of lions means that the apostles are the leaders of the Christians. * Fire comes out of their mouth means the tongues of fire, which came out of the apostles. The third of men means that the third (of men), who refused to believe in the preaching of the apostles, disappeared from the world.

17. The symbolism of the word "lion". - The prophets and the apostles were called lions. The Unique Son was called lion. Satan is likened to the lion; not that we compare him to Our Lord, but it is because of all his strength that he was nicknamed lion. Indeed, the Apostle said: For your enemy is the demon, who roars like a lion, and seeks whom to devour. As to how Our Savior was called lion, listen to what the book of * Job says: The roaring of the lion and the cry of the lioness. His roaring means 'Elohe, 'Elohe (words

pronounced) on the wood of the cross. The lioness is the Holy Church, which cries, saying: By the blood of his side he baptized me. Jacob says: You will sleep and you will rise like a lion, while no one will wake you. When you hear the prophecy of Jacob, let it not seem to you (said) concerning Judah, his son. It means on the contrary that he prophesied concerning the Son. As to how the prophets were called lions, the prophet Nahum says: Where are the dens of the lions and where do the cubs of the lions graze? Where are the dens of the lions? means that * the ancient Fathers and the prophets descended into Sheol. Where do the cubs of the lions graze? means the children of the prophets. Where did the lion go? means Christ, who descended into Sheol. When Bartholomew had said (to the Lord): O Lord, where did you go, when you descended from your cross? the Lord Jesus said to him: I descended immediately into Sheol, in order to save the Fathers.

Nahum said clearly: The cub of the lion has entered there. || He said again: His side is full of what he has caught. This (word) means that, when he had caught Satan, his side was filled with his glorious blood. When the centurion had struck (Jesus), blood and water flowed. * It is then that we were marked as faithful. Those who make a treasure of faith were marked by the strength of faith. hesitant, does not know the negations, (made) by the objections of the demons. His necklace is tied by a knot, which will not undo; he put it on the neck of his spouse. This means that (Christ) put (a necklace) around the neck of the holy Church, and baptized her with the water of the great gift of his grace. The Evangelists were called horns, as Zechariah said: I saw four workers, who were sharpening horns. These are the four patriarchs, who commented on the Gospels. That is why John said: The word was heard from the horns of the altar. * The altar means the holy Church."

18. The wound of the beast. - When the angel had exposed all this to me, I said to our Father John: Expose to me the explanation of your word. He said to me: Concerning what are you asking (me)? I said (to him): Concerning the beast, one of whose heads was pierced, and also concerning the word, which was said: From its mortal wound it is healed. The Apostle said: The beast means Satan. One of whose heads was pierced means that his kingdom was pierced by the cross of Jesus Christ. It died, all the work of Satan, which had reigned in the world, before the coming of the Son, and it was destroyed by the death of Christ. From its * mortal wound it is healed means the kingdom of the false Messiah, who will come on the good pleasure of Satan. 19. A means A233*+ (demons); A means N2M3 (Satan); © means +77 (persecutor); ? means &2; N means NA+C (Belial); A means 4,P-N%N (devil). The book of Revelation says again: The second angel came. * He was carrying the Gospel. This one means John the Baptist. (Revelation) tells you clearly: He was flying and he was preaching. He was saying: The hour of judgment has arrived. This means that the kingdom of heaven is near. The second angel, who was saying: Babylon has fallen, it is the Son, who said: The world has fallen. The third angel means Peter, the prince of the apostles. Blessed are those who died because of the word of the Lord means the apostles. I saw a glass, which resembled the sea and was mixed with fire. The glass means the varied asceticism of the saints. The sea means the Church. Those who will have conquered the beast will stand * on the sea means those who will have believed and will have been baptized.

The second angel poured out his vial into the sea. The second angel means Joshua, son of Nun. The sea became blood, and every living soul that was in the sea died. The sea is the world. Blood means the great slaughter that took place in the days of Joshua. Every (soul) died means the extermination of Canaan. No one was left, except the people of Gibeon, who, being far away, used ruse. At Jericho, Rahab was left. Joshua is explained by the Law and by the prophets, who contemplated the coming of the Lord. Rahab is explained by the Church of the nations. The harlot * means the fornication of the nations. It is Rahab who tied the red sign to the window of her house.

As for the twelve stones that Joshua chose when (the Israelites) had crossed the river Jordan, they are explained by the twelve apostles. One day became two days means that such an event occurred during the crucifixion of Our Lord, that is to say the day of Friday,

The third (angel), who poured out his vial into the rivers and the fountains of water, is explained by the prophet Samuel. The rivers mean the house of Israel. Blood means the sacrifice of the Law. The fountains of water mean that the Law was a fountain of water. The angel of the fountains of water means the priest Eli. They poured out the blood of your prophets means the blood of the sacrifice, (with) which the prophets were sprinkled. As for the children of Eli: Hophni and Phinehas, seeing that they defiled the sacrifice; that the prescription of lamentations (during) the sacrifice was abandoned; that they poured out the blood, without sprinkling, * (during) the sacrifice: in exchange for the fact that they poured out the blood without sprinkling, their blood was poured out, and they were pierced through the mouth. That is why the Scripture says: You have made them drink of their blood, for (that) was just for them. This exposition of the mysteries, which (the Lord) gave me, is hidden. No one among men can explain it, nor recount it.

that the prophet Elijah chained the heaven. (The Apocalypse) tells it to you clearly: It became hot and men were burned by fire. Fire means hunger, for hunger is the fire of the belly. Men blasphemed * the Lord. The house of Ahab denied the Creator. The fifth (angel) poured out his vial upon the throne (of the beast). He who poured out the vial means he who expounded the prophecy: Isaiah, son of Amoz. The beast is Manasseh, who exercised his fury against Isaiah. His kingdom became dark means that he went to the land of Babylon in captivity.

The sixth (angel) poured out his vial into the river Euphrates. The river Euphrates means the house of Israel. He who poured out the vial is Ezekiel. Its water dried up. The water means the word of the Lord, which ceased in Israel. In order that the way of the kings * of the East might be prepared means the kings of Persia. The seventh angel poured out his vial into the wind means John the Baptist. The wind means the multitude of the nations. The water dried up means that John the Baptist became the term and the end of the prophets. Just as John became the one who closes the choir of the prophets, so at the seventh millennium the world will end."

the book of Clement, expound to me," I said to the Apostle. He said to me: "O means 'Esewos, who killed James, the brother of Our Lord Jesus; A means Aaron * Il; M means Tiberius II, son of the son of Tiberius; + means Carinus, king of Nineveh; means Kabir, king of Persia; H means Segar, son of Ator, king of Ne'mar, (descendant) of the sons of Esau; A means Aelianus, who condemned George, martyr; TP means Maximian; A means Agrippa, that is to say Diocletian; 7 means Megelines, who was king of Antioch; A means Etahé, king of Rome, who was famous; O means Qaribin, king of Kuergi who is of the lineage * of Japheth. When he had risen for war at the time of sowing, he perished at (the time of) the harvest. This exposition is wonderful and prodigious.

in whose days the crosses were lacking. He took to the sea, to (go) look for them.” 22. The false Messiah. - (The Scripture) also tells you: The king of Teman will rise, who resembles the ox, that is to say the false Messiah, who is like Christ. He will rise, saying: I am the Christ. The cub of the lion means the king of Rome. He himself will meet the king of Ethiopia. Then will come the time of happiness. Then will be abundant the cereals, the honey, the oil, the milk of the cattle. Men will weep in the tombs. * One will say to the other: Such a one, rise up; come here, for the cereals, the honey, and the oil are abundant. The cereals, which will have been sown in small quantity, will become abundant. The clothes, with which man will be covered, will last seven years. I have expounded to your intelligence all this, which will come to pass. As for this exposition, it is a mystery.

23. The hidden Scriptures. - Concerning the Scriptures of the New Law, which are hidden, listen as I expound to you. (There are) four of the Holy Gospel, as the Scripture says: There are other (things), which the Lord Jesus did; many things, which were not written in this book; twelve, it is said, of Peter, as he says himself: To us nothing * was revealed, except a few things; four of Paul, as he says himself: I heard an exposition, which

is not for men; seven of John, as he recounted himself: By the seven series is explained the sound of the seven thunders, as we said just now; one of James. Behold: in all, twenty-eight. 24. The hidden mystery. - This exposition of the mystery was given to men (destined to receive) the mysteries, as the Lord said: My mysteries have been given to mine. Do not reveal this book to every man you meet, except to the doctors and the intelligent men. The sons of the prophet Moses kept the little book that their father had left them. As for the children * of his son, when they had revealed this book to every man, then the angel came, snatched it, and made it ascend to the heavens. It is to you that I have said this exposition. Keep this important mystery, and do not reveal how you found it. 25. The reading of the book. - Having said this, the Apostle ascended (to heaven). But the angel remained to teach me all this mystery. He himself said to me: No peace. Then I wept much. The angel himself said to me: Do not weep. No being, endowed with a body, has seen your dwelling. Because of the word of the Lord, which you have sought, your reward will be considerable in the heavens. Indeed, the light of the body is the eye, (but) * the light of the soul is the word of the Lord. Having heard this, he rejoiced and held a great feast. 40,000,000 children rejoiced with him. He will become blessed


[p. 413] {#po.6.p413}
ይቤሉ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ወይድኅን ፡አመንሱት ፡ ወኢይትሀጐል ፡ ወበሰማያትኒ ፡ ይ ነሥእ ፣ ፅሜቶ ። ወዝንቱ ፣ መጽሐፍ ፡ ይትነበብ ፡ እምሰኑየ ፡ ሕማማት ፡ በሰሙን ፡ በአ ምጣነ ፡ ይክሉ ፡ ያንብብዎ ። ወበበዓለ ፣ ዮሐንስኒ ፡ ዘርእየ ፡ ዘንተ ፣ ምሥጢረ ። ወበ በዓለ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ታቦርሂ ። ወበዓለ ፡ ሚካኤልሂ ፡ ዘሕዳር። ወበዓለ ፡ ዓር8 ፡ እንስሳሂ ። ወ አለ ፡ ዓዕለውሰ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ መጽሐፍ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘትትረከብ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፣ በሀገረ ፣ አንተ ፡ ይእቲ' ፣ ወካዕ"”በ ፣ ዓዲ ፡ በድንዙና ። ወተዝካሩሰ ፡ ለአባ ፡ በጸሎተ ፡ ሚካኤ ል = አመ ፡ ጳለኅዳር ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ተሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ስብሐት ፡ ወክብር ፡ ይደ
whoever reads this book. Whoever reads this book will be saved from peril, will not perish, and will receive his reward in the heavens. This book will be read from the day after the Passion, for a week, - (one will read) as much as one can read - at the feast of John, who saw this mystery, at the feast of Mount Tabor, at the feast of Michael in Hedar, and at the feast of the Four Animals. Those who will translate this book will say: It was not found, except in the city of … that is to say … and * also at Denkuena. The memory of Abba Batsalota Mika'el is the 7th of Hedar. May his prayer be with us! Amen. Glory and honor befit the Trinity! Amen. Amen.

### III. - THE DIVINITY AND THE CREATION (THIRD TREATISE)
ሉ ፡ለስላሴ ፡ አሜን፡ ዐያሚ'፣፳ ቀዳሚ ፡ ነገር ፣ በእንተ ፣ ምስጢረ ፡ መለኮት ፡ ኢሀሎ ፡ አብ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ወልድ ፡፡
ወኢሀሎ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ አቡሁ ።፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስኒ ፡ ኢሀሎ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፣፡ ፳ውእቱ ፡ ሀልዎቶሙ ፣ እምቅድመ ፡ ይትፈጠር ፡ ዓለም ፡= ወጸሐልዎቶሙ ፡ እምድኅረ ፣ ፍጥረተ ፣ ዓለም ። አልቦ" ፣ አመ ፡ ኢሀለው ፣ ወአልቦ ፡ አመ ፡ ኢይሄል ው= ወአልቦ ፡ አመ ፡ ይብልዎሙ ፡ እምዘ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ሀለው ።፡ ወእስከ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ይ
1. The mystery of the Divinity. - First exposition concerning the mystery of the Divinity. The Father did not exist before the Son. The Son did not exist before his Father. The Holy Spirit did not exist before the Father and the Son. One was their existence, before the world was created. One is their existence, after the creation of the world. Never have they (ceased to) exist. Never will they (cease to) exist. Never will one say of them: "Since they existed thus, and until they exist thus." Their existence had no beginning.


[p. 414] {#po.6.p414}
ሄልው ፡፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ጥንተ ፡ ሀልዎቶሙ ፡ ወአልቦ ፣ ማሕለቅተ ፣ ሀልዎቶሙ ፡-እንዘ፣ ፳፻ ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ ያጸስብሐት ፡ ሎ”ቱ ፡ ለመፍቀሬ ፡ ሰብእ ።
Their existence will have no end. Being one, they are three. Being three, they are one. Glory * to the Friend of men!
ኦባሕረ ፡ ምሕረት ፣ ወትሕትና ፡=፡ ልማዱሰ ፡ ለሰብእ ፣፡ ኅጢአት ፡፡ ወልማዱ ፡ ለእ ግዚአብሔር ፡ ምሕረት ፡፡ ልማደ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ አበሳ ፡፡ ወልማዱሰ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ሣሀ ል ፡ ወሕድገተ ፡ አበላ ቦ ፡ ዘፈቀደ ፡ ያከብሮ ፣ ወለዘፈቀደ ፡ ያኃስሮ ፡=፡ ለዘፈቀደ ፡ ያ ልዕሎ ፡ ወለዘፈቀደ ፣ ያትሕቶ ፡=፡ ሕድግሰ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ ወእምንዋየ ፡ ልሕኩትኒ ፡ ቦ ፡ ዘአ ክበረ ፡ ወቦ ፡ ዘአሕሰረ ፡፡ ቦ ፡ ዘረሰየ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፣ ሙዳየ ፡ መዓር ፡ ወቅብእ ፡፡ ወቦ 0፡ ዘረሰየ ፡ እምውስቴቱ ፡ ዘይዘበጥ ፡ በእሳት ፡፡ ወእምነ ፡ ዕፅኒ ፡ ወእምነ ፣ እብንኒ ፡ ቦ ፡ ዘ አዕበየ ፣ ወቦ ፡ ዘቀደሰ ፣ ወቦ ፡ ዘ[አ]ዓሠረኒ ፡=፡ ይትባረክ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ነፋሳትኒ ፡ ሰረገላሁ ፡ ወደብተ”ራ ፡ እሳት ፣ ሐይመታቲሁ ፡፡ ወስዕለ ፡ ማይ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልብሱ ፡፡ ደመ ናትኒ ፡ ጸበለ ፡ እገሪሁ ።=፡ ወማየ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ኢየአክል ፡ ሕፍኑ ። ወውአቱ ፡ ሰረራ ፡ ለባ ሕር ፡ በአሕባል ፡ ወኮነ ፡ መጠና ፡ ምሕዋረ ፡ ፲፻ዓመት ።=፡ ወይእቲ ፡ ተሰምየት ፣ምክ ያደ ፡ እግሩ' ። ወዓዲ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ርአሰ ፡ እንስሳሁ ፡ ስፉሕ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ዘባሕረ ፡ በረድ ፡ ኅ በ ፣፡ይነብር ፣ ወልድ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አቡሁ ፣ ወምስለ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ" = ወእሙንቱ ፡ይ ሴብሕዎ ፡ በመፍርህ ፡ መንጦላዕት ፡፡
O sea of mercy and humility! The habit of man is sin. The habit of the Lord is mercy. The habit of man is fault. The habit of the Lord is clemency and the remission of the fault. He honors whom he wills. He debases whom he wills. He raises whom he wills. He lowers whom he wills. All the more reason (does he treat) men (thus). Among the pottery vessels, he makes some precious, but he makes others vile. He makes of some of them a container for honey (or) for oil. He makes of others (containers), in which (metals) are treated by fire. Among the woods and the stones also, he makes some noble, he makes some holy, but he makes others vile. May his name be blessed! The winds are his chariots. * His tent is a tabernacle of fire. Water is represented on his garment. The clouds are the dust of his feet. The water of the sea does not suffice for his fist. He himself founded the sea, (limiting it) with cords. Its extent is that of a journey of a thousand years. It itself is called the footstool of his feet. Furthermore, upon the head of his animals is stretched the heaven of the sea of crystal, where the Son dwells with his Father and with the Holy Spirit. (Beings) glorify him behind a terrible veil.


[p. 415] {#po.6.p415}
እሎንቱሰ ፡ አፍራስ ፡ አለ ፡ ስኡላን ፡ በነደ ፡ እሳት ።፡ ወነኩርኳሮሙኒ ፡ መርእድ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ይሰምዑ ፡ንስቲተ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ፈጣሪ ፡ ይርዕዱ ፣ ወይደነግፁ ። ወ ይቤሎሙ ፡ ፈጣ ሪ፣ ኢትፍርሁ ፣ ወኢትይንግፁ ፡ ጹሩኒ ፡ እንዘ ፡ አነ ፡ አጸውረክሙ ። ወእምብዝኀ ፡ ፍሥሓ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ፈቀዱ ፡ ሰሪረ ፣ ይትነሣእ ፣ ሰረገላ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ወይበጽሕ ፡ ምስሌሆ ሙ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ወይትሌዓል ፡ እስከ ፡ ሰማያት ።
As for the horses, which are represented by the flame of fire, their whirling is terrifying. When they hear a little the voice of the Creator, they tremble and they are terrified. * The Creator says to them: Do not be afraid and do not be terrified. Carry me, since I myself carry you. When, in the intensity of (their) joy, they want to leap, (their) chariot arises with them. It arrives with them to earth and rises up to the heavens.
ወአልቦ ፡ ጋእዘ ፣ ማአከሌሆሙ ፣ ወኢያአምሩ ፡ ተጋእዞ ፣ ወተካህዶ ፡=፡ ወልቦሙ ፡ ስፉሕ ፡ ለምሕረት ፡ ከመ ፡ አምላኮሙ ፣ ወማዕጠንቶሙ ፡ ይስእል ፡ ለምሕረት ፣ ወመ ስቀሎሙ ፡ ይስእል ፣ ለምሕረት ።፡ ወመሥዋዕቶሙ ፡ ይስእል ፣ ለምሕረት ። ወታቦቶ ሙኒ ፡ ይስዕል ፡ ለምሕረት ፡፡ ወማዕጠንቶሙ ፡፣ ዘውእቱ ፡ ጸሎተ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡፡ ወሶበ ፣ ያዐርጉ ፡ ፅጣነ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ እሳት ፣ "ማሕየዊ ፡ ለአለ ፣ የአምኑ ፣ በስመ ፣ ዚ አሁ ። ወእሳተ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ለአለ ፡ ኢየአምኑ ፡ ቦቱ ።፡ ወውእቱ ፡ አሳት ፡ ወፅአ ፡ እምሕ ፅነ ፡ አሳት ፡፡ ወባሕርይ ፡ ክቡር ፡=፡
There is no quarrel between (the angels). They do not know chicanery, nor dispute. Their heart is open to mercy, like (that of) their God. Their censer asks for mercy. Their cross asks for mercy. Their sacrifice asks for mercy. Their ark asks for mercy. Their censer means the prayer of the saints. When they offer incense, they say: Glory. There is a vivifying fire * for those who believe in the name (of the Lord), and a fire of torment for those who do not believe in it. The fire itself comes out from the bosom of the fire. It is a noble substance.

2. The Trinity. - The third (mystery) is not less than the two (preceding). It is still the power of the Trinity. Listen: I am going to expound (it) to you. The Father said: Let us make man in our image and in our likeness. The Son said: I myself will clothe myself in the flesh of Adam. The Holy Spirit said: I myself will dwell in the heart of the prophets and of the apostles, in the heart of

all the saints, who will believe in me with an upright heart and with a pure intelligence. The divinity forms with the Incarnation one (single) essence. The Father did not abandon the essence of the Father, nor the Son the essence of the Son. The Holy Spirit is * equal to them. The Father is the sender of the sent Son. The Holy Spirit is pleased in this work. The Father is begetter, but not begotten. The Son is begotten, but not begetter. The Holy Spirit is pleased in their own wisdom. They are rulers and no one rules them. They are judges and no one judges them. They knew man, before creating him. As for them, no one knows them thoroughly. They are killers and no one can kill them. Who knows if they kill or if they make someone live?

3. The creation of heaven and earth. - After this exposition, the luminous (angel) answered and said to my father Bahayela Mika'el: Listen as I expound to you the mystery of the explanation of the creation of heaven and earth. The work * which was done the first (day), is the image of the last of the days (and) of the coming of the Lord. Sunday is the image of the new day of the resurrection (of the Lord). The works which were created on Sunday are the image of the clergy of the Church. See then that the Trinity is in the Law, (which) expounds and says: In the beginning the Lord made the heaven. The beginning means Christ, as the prophet Isaiah says: I am the first and I am the last. John said: He is the first. Paul said: The first is the

Christ. Furthermore, John the Evangelist told you: In the beginning was the Word. Mark the Evangelist said: * Beginning of the Gospel of Jesus Christ, Son of the Lord. Concerning the Holy Spirit the Law expounds to you clearly and tells (you): The Spirit of the Lord was hovering over the water. See then that the Trinity is clearly at the head of the Law. The earth existed from the origin. That means the Church, which existed from the origin in the heart of the Lord. Darkness was above the abyss. Darkness means the image of the society of the Jews. The abyss is the image of the Law. The pure heaven is the image of the Apostles, as * they said themselves: Widening our mouth as large as the heaven, we pray. Fire is the image of the fire of the vivifying life. Water is the image of baptism. The wind is the image of the Holy Spirit. The Holy Spirit is the sanctifier of every creature. The holy angels are the image of the priests. Let there be light is what (the Lord) said at the beginning. It is the image of the Word. That means that whoever will have believed and will have been baptized will be saved.

Law is the image of the Gospel. The Gospel is the image of the Word, which calls the just, saying: Come to me, the blessed of my Father, * in order to inherit the kingdom, which was prepared for you. Whoever will not have believed will be damned. That means the image of the Word. Go, cursed, (to the fire) which was prepared for Satan and for his angels. The tabernacle of Israel is the shadow of the Church. The Church is the shadow of the heavenly Jerusalem. The Law is the shadow of the holy Gospel. The Gospel is the shadow of the new world. The prophets are the shadow of the apostles. The apostles are the shadow of the (living who lead) the life of the angels. The priests of the Law are the shadow of the priests of the Gospel. The priests of the Gospel are the shadow of the priests of heaven. However, the priests of the Gospel surpass in authority the priests of heaven. These, indeed, do not take the Mystery, * except with tongs of fire. But the priests of the Gospel take it with their hands. That is why the Lord said: Adam has become like one of us. Perhaps he will raise his hands, will eat (of the fruit) of the tree of life, and will live forever. Like one of us: one means the Only-Begotten Son. Perhaps he will raise his hands means the priests, who raise their hands to eat the flesh of the Son. Truly then the tree of life is Christ himself, as he has


[p. 419] {#po.6.p419}
ለሊሁ ፡ አነ ፡ ውአቱ ፡ ፅፀ ፡ ሕይወት ።፡፡ ዘበልዑ ፡ አዳም ፡ ወሔዋን ። እስመ ፡ ምሥጢ ር ፡ኅቡእ ፡ ኅደረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልበ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ አሚነ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመን ፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡=፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ ፍካሬ ፡ ፍጥረት ፡ ዘእጉድ ፡ ሰንበ”ት ወበዕለተ ፡ ሰኑይሂ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ ፅርገቱ ፣ ለእግዚእነ ፣ ይእቲ ።፡፡ በከመ ፡ በሰኑይ ፡ዐ ርገ ፡ ማይ ፡ ወከማሁ ፡ እግዚእነሂ ፡ ማየ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ዐርገ ፡ ሰማያተ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ አርብ ዓ ፡፡ ማይሰ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ይተረጐም ፡ ቦ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ይተረጐም ፡ ኅበ ፡ አሕዛብ ። ወቦ ፣ አመ ፡ ይተረጐም ፡ ኅበ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወናሁ ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ምሥጢር ፡ ዘሰኑይ = ኑ
said himself: I am the tree of life, (of the fruit) of which Adam and Eve ate. Indeed, a hidden mystery dwells in the heart of man means faith in the Father, in the Son, and in the Holy Spirit. The explanation of the work of the first day of the week is finished. * On the second day, it is the symbol of the Ascension of Our Lord. Just as on the second day the water rose, so Our Lord also, water of life, ascended to the heavens, on the fortieth day. Water is explained by many (things); somewhere it is explained by the nations; sometimes it is explained by the Church. Behold, the exposition of the mystery of the second day is finished.
ወዕለተ ፡ ሠሉስኒ ፣ አምሳለ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ፃይእቲ ፡ አስመ ፡ ሠሉስ ፡ በሥላሴ ፡ ያር ኢ ። ወበሠሉስ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለይትጋባእ ፡ ማይ ። ውስተ ፡ ፡ ወዝኒ ፡ ብ ሂል ፡ ይትጋብኡ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ በ፳ሃይማኖት ፡፡ ወየብስኒ ፡ ጽንዓ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ውአቱ ። ወፅፀውኒ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ ያርኢ ፡ ትር ጓሜሁ ።፡፡ ወ"”"ሐመልማልኒ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደቂቆሙ ፡ ለሐዋርያት ፡ ያርኢ ።፡ ወደቂቆሙኒ ፡ ለ ሐዋርያት ፡ እለ ፤ አምኑ ፡ በእደዊሆሙ ።፡ ወዘርእኒ ፡ እሉ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ አርድእት ፡እ ለ፣ ዘርኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገጸ ፡ ምድር ።፡ በበዘርኡ ፣ ዘይቤሂ ፣ በበክፍሎሙ ፡ ወበበ ፡ ስብ ከቶሙ ፡፡ ወገነትኒ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ይተረጐም ።፡ ወጽጌያቲሃ ፡ ወሕንባባቲሃኒ ፡
The third day is the symbol of the fiftieth day. Indeed, the third day indicates the Trinity. The third day, the Lord said: Let the water gather into one (place). That means that the nations will gather toward (the Lord) in one same faith. The dry land is the strength of the faith. The interpretation of the trees indicates the apostles. * The green (plants) indicate the children of the apostles. The children of the apostles are those who believed thanks to them. The seed is the disciples, who sowed on the surface of the earth. Each with his seed means each with his part, each with his preaching. The garden is explained by the Church. The flowers and the berries of the garden are explained by the people.


[p. 420] {#po.6.p420}
ለገነት ፡ ኀበ ፣ ሕዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ይተረጐም ። ወዘርእኒ ፣ ዘበበዘመዱ ፡ ወበበአምሳ ሊሁ ፡ ወበበዘመዱ ።፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ዘመዱ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ እስራኤል ፡ እሙንቱ ፡እለ፣ አምኑ ፡ ቦቱ ፡=፡ ወክርስቲያንሂ ፡ አለ ፡ አምኑ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ እሙንቱሂ ፡ ዘመዱ ፡ ለአግዚአብ ሔር ። ወካክዕበ ፡ዘርእሰ ፡ቃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወይከውን ፡ መብልዐ ፡ ለአዕዋ ፈ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ዘይቤሂ ፡ አፅዋፍ ፡ እንከ ፡ ይ”ተረጐሙ ፣ መላእክት ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወእሉኒ ፡ አለ ፡ አምኑ ፡ አማን ፡ በቃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘተሰብከ ፡ በታሕተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወይቤሉ ፣
of Christians. As for the seeds, they differ each according to their family, according to their type, and according to their species. The first family of the Lord is Israel, which believed in him. Christians, who believe in the Lord, are also the family of the Lord. Furthermore, the seed is the word of the Lord. It becomes the food of the birds of the sky. The birds are therefore * explained by the angels of heaven. These are those who have truly believed in the word of the Lord, which was preached under the sky. They said: Glory to Him who loves mankind.

5. The four rivers. - The four rivers are explained by the four Evangelists. The first river is the Pishon. There is found the green gold and the precious gems, which shine. There is found the red gold. Such is therefore the explanation that Abba Bahayela Mikä'êl set forth to me, to me, his son, Isaac. I have written (this), so that wisdom might come to posterity. The first river is the image of the book of the Gospel, which Matthew wrote. The green gold is the Incarnation, which Our Lord took from Mary, as says * the prophet Moses: I saw a green shrub. Fire was mingled with it. This is therefore the divinity of Our Lord. There are found the precious gems means baptism. On the day of baptism, the name of the man reaches the golden column. The name is recorded in the portion of the angel, and inscribed in the

portion of the angel who is joined (to the man), on the day of baptism. When he takes up the fast, the angel of the fast joins him, on Friday and Wednesday. When he becomes merciful, the angel of mercy joins him. [He] will make him reach the Angel of life, that is to say (to) Jesus Christ, the Only-Begotten Son. Indeed, (Jesus) will say to his Father: Have pity on my people, who are marked with the blood of my side. * Truly (the Father) will have pity (on the people).
ሕር፤0ሎቱ፣ ወዘይወፅእ ፡ እምነ ፡ ፈለግ ፡ ፍካሬሁ ፡ ያርኢ ፡-ኅበ ፡ ሕፃናት ፡ እለ ፣ ቀተሎሙ ፡ ሄሮድስ ፣ ወአማን ፣ ቀዬሓሐን ፡ እሉ ፡ በደመ ፡ ስምዕ ፡፡ ወካክልዕ ፡ ፈለግ ፣ ግዮን ፡ ዘይቤ ሂ ፡ ያርኢ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ክልዕ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ዘጸሐፈ ፡ ማርቆስ ፡ ኦደ ፣ ውስተ ፡ አድያ መ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ወኢትዮጵያ ።፡ ወሣልስ ፡ ፈለግ ፡ ጤግሮስ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ውእቱኬ ፡ አርአ ያሁ ፡ ለብሔረ ፡ ወንጌል ፤ ዘጸሐፈ ፡ ሉቃስ ፣ ወያአውድ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ፋርስ" ፡ ፋርስሰ ፤ ዝዓለም ፡ ውእቱ ። ወፋርስኒ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ እሳት ፡ ብሂል ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ኢሳይያስ ፡ ወያ ውዒ ፡ በፋርስ ፡ ኀይል ። ወራብዕ ፡ ፈለግ ፡ ኤፍራጥስ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘያዓውድ ፡ ዮርዳኖ ስ ፡ አምሳሊሁ ፡ ለብሔረ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ዝዘጸሐፈ ፡ ዮሐን”ስ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ነባቢ ፡ መለኮት ፡=፡ ወዮሐንስ ፡ ጸሐፈ ፡ በነገረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ ለሰብአ ፣፡ ኤፌሶን ፡ እስመ ፡ ኤፌሶን ፡ ሮሜ፣፡ይ እቲ ፡ ወሉቃስኒ ፡ ጸሐፈ ፡ በነገረ ፡ ሮሜ ። ወማርቆስኒ ፡ በቅብጢ ፡፡ ወማቴዎስኒ ፡ በዕ ብራይስጢ ።፡
6. Explanation of the rivers. - (Here is) the explanation that emerges from the rivers. (The first river) indicates the children whom Herod killed. Truly these (children) are red with the blood of martyrdom. The second river is the Gihon. It indicates the second book of the Gospel that Mark wrote. It is around the territory of Egypt and Ethiopia. The third river is the Tigris, that is to say, therefore, the symbol of the book of the Gospel, which Luke wrote. It surrounds the land of Persia. Persia is this world. Persia also means fire, as Isaiah says: (The fire) will consume Persia (with) force. The fourth river is the Euphrates, which surrounds (the land of) Jordan. It is the symbol of the book of the Gospel that John wrote, * who is the theologian of the divinity. John wrote in the language of Rome to the people of Ephesus. Indeed, Ephesus is Rome. Luke also wrote in the language of Rome. Mark (wrote) in Coptic; Matthew in Hebrew.


[p. 422] {#po.6.p422}
ወኤፌሶን ፡ ትውኅዝ ፡ ሐሊበ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፣ ትምህርቱ ፡ ለኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ እግዚ እነ = ወግዮን ፡ ትውሕዝ ፡ ወይን ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ፍሥሓ ፡ ትንሣኤሁ ።፡ ወጤግሮስ ፣ ትው ሕዝ ፣ መዓር ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ አምሳለ ፣ ትዕግሥቱ ፣ ወሕማማቲሁ ።፡ ወኤፍራጦስ ፡ ት ውሕዝ ፡ ቅብዓ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ መዊዕ ፡ ዘመለኮት ።፡ ወዝውእቱ ፡ ፈለግ ፡ ርስቶ ሙ ፡ለጻድቃን ፡ወለሕፃናት ፡=፡ ወእለ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ሕድጋተ' ፡ አበሳ ፡ ይሰትዩ ፡ አምፈለገ፡ ሐ ሊብ ፡5፡ ወአለ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ጸዊረ ፡ ነገ”ር ፡ ይሰትዩ ፡ እምፈለገ ፡ መዓር ፡=፡ ወአለ ፡ ቦሙ፣፡ ፍ ሥሓ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ይሰትዩ ፡ እምፈለገ ፡ ወይን ፡=፡ ወእለ ፡ መጠው ፡ ነፍሶሙ ፡ በእንተ 0፣
(From) the Pishon flows milk, that is to say the doctrine of Jesus Christ Our Lord. (From) the Gihon flows wine, that is to say the joy of his resurrection. (From) the Tigris flows honey, that is to say the symbol of his patience and his sufferings. (From) the Euphrates flows oil, that is to say the symbol of the victory of the divinity. It is the river of the inheritance of the righteous and the children. Those who have repudiated sin will drink from the river of milk. Those who have had the charge of the word * will drink from the river of honey. Those who have had the joy of the spirit will drink from the river of wine. Those who have surrendered their soul for the sake of their neighbor will drink from the river of oil.
ቢጸጾሙ ፡ ይሰትዩ ፡ እምፈለገ ፡ ቅብዕ ፡፡ ወዘንተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘከርነ ፡ እንበይነ ፡ ግብር ፡ ዘተፈጥረ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ሠሉስ ። ወኩሉ ፡ ነገር ፡ ዘሠሉስ ፡ ወፍክሬሁ ፡ በምሥጢር ፡ ተፈጸመ ።፡ ወበረቡዕ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ይኩን ፡ፀሐ ይ ፡ ወወርኅ ፡ ወከዋክብት ፡ ወዕለተ ፡ ረቡዕ ፡ እንከ ፣ አምሳለ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ታቦር ።፡ ወወር ኅኒ ፡ አርአያሁ ፡ ለጴጥሮስ ፡ ወለዙሎሙ ፡ አኅዊሁ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፣ ወከዋክብትኒ ፡ አርአ ያሆሙ ፡ ለዥሎሙ ፡ ጻድቃን ።፡፡ ወዘመሰልናሃ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ደ ብረ ፡ ታቦር ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስኖሙ ፡ ለፀሐይ ፤ ወወርኅ ፡ ወከዋክብት ። እስመ ፡ ከመ ፡ ፀሐ ይ ፡ ምክዕቢተ ፡ ይበርሁ ፣ ጸድቃን ።፡=፡ ወዝንቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ "ነገረ ፡ ፍካሬ ፡ ዘበእንተ ፣ ረቡዕ።
7. The days of creation. - We have mentioned all this regarding the work that was created on the third day. The entire exposition of the third day and the explanation of the mystery are finished. On the fourth day, he said: Let there be the sun, the moon, and the stars. The fourth day is therefore the symbol of the mountain of Tabor. The moon is the image of Peter and all his brothers, the apostles. The stars are the image of all the righteous. We compare this day to the day of Mount Tabor, because of the beauty of the sun, the moon, and the stars. Indeed, the righteous will shine twice as the sun. This is * the exposition of the explanation that relates to the fourth day.


[p. 423] {#po.6.p423}

On the fifth day, he said: Let the water produce the birds, the fish, the sea monsters, and the two (monsters) which are called Behemoth and Leviathan. The fifth day is therefore explained by the prayer of Thursday, in which Our Lord showed the firstfruits of his flesh and his blood. The birds, which came out on the fifth day, are explained by the apostles, to whom he gave his flesh, on Thursday evening. The fish are explained by the holy Fathers, who played in Sheol, and came out, (delivered) by the blood of his side. The sea monsters indicate Satan and the society of the Jews. The animals indicate the feast of the saints. Such is the exposition and such is the explanation of the fifth day. What relates to Friday is a mystery. On Friday were created the beasts, the animals, and the reptiles, * that is to say the creeping things. Adam was created after all the beasts. This is therefore explained by the princes of the priests and the society of the Jews, who gathered in Jerusalem on Friday.
አለ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ዓርብ ፡ ወእንስሳሂ ፡ ይተረጐሙ ፡ በአሕዛብ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኀረዮሙ ፣ በዲበ ፡ መስቀል ፣ ወከመ ሰ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ ተሰምዩ ፡ እንስሳ =፡ ይቤ ፡ እቤ" ፡ ኢሳይያስ ፡ ነገር ፡ በእንተ ፡ እንስሳ ፡ገ ዳም ፡ በእንት ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ ብሂል ።፡ ወምክያስኒ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ከመ ፡ እንስሳ ፡ በማእከለ ፡ እን ስሳ ፡ዘገዳም ፡፳፡ ዝኒ ፡ ብሒል ፡ ከመ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በማእከለ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ ብሂል ፡፡ ወዕዝራ
The animals are explained by the peoples. Indeed, (Jesus) chose them on the cross. As to how the peoples were called animals, Isaiah said: I make an exposition regarding the animals of the desert, that is to say regarding the peoples. Micah said that the (domestic) animals are in the midst of the animals of the desert. This very thing means that Christ was in the midst of the peoples.


[p. 424] {#po.6.p424}

Ezra said: Rise up, O lion, (coming) from the desert. As to how the society of the Jews was called (a herd of) beasts, listen to what John the Baptist said: Brood of vipers, who has shown you * The prophets said: The serpents are (the beasts) that lick the dust. Lick the dust means the defection of those who have abandoned the Creator. The beings that move mean the family of reptiles, which was created against Adam, (and) which dwells on the earth, as David said: The sea is great; there are the innumerable reptiles. The sea is the world. The reptiles are the peoples.
ስኒ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ እሙንቱ። ወከመ ፡ ምንትኑ ፡ አዳም ፡ ተፈጥረ ፡ ድኅረ ፣ ኩሉ ፡ በከመ ፡፣ አዳም ፡ ተፈጥረ ፣ በፅለተ ፣ ዓርብ = ወከማሁ ፣ እግዚእነሂ ፡ ዓርገ ፣ ዲበ ፡ ዕፀ ፣ መስቀል ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ዓር ብ ወይቤ ፡ ይኩኑ ፡ ጸሹኩሎሙ ፡፣ ወአዳምሰ ፣ ተፈጥረ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ዐርብ ፡ እንከ ፡ አርአ ያሃ ፡ ለስቅለተ ፡ እግዚእ ፣ ይእቲ ፡=፡ ወእምኔሁ ፡ ሠረፁ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ "ሶበ ፡ ጸለየ ፡ በ ዲበ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ይኩኑ ፡ 8ሹሎሙ ። ወአዳምሰ ፣ ቀፈጥረ ፡ በ፪ጠባይዕ ፡ወ ክርስቶስኒ ፣ ደመረ ፡ መለኮቶ ፡ ማዕከለ ፣ ትርቡዓን ።፡ ወዕለተ ፡ ዓርብሰ ፡ አርአያነሃ ፡ እ ንከ ፡ ለስቅለተ ፡ እግዚአ ።፡ ወበይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፣ ሶበ ፣ ውሕዘ ፡ ደም ፡ ወማይ ፡ እምገቦ
8. Creation of Adam. - How is it that Adam was created after all (the beings)? Just as Adam was created on Friday, so Our Lord ascended the wood of the cross on Friday. He said: Let them all be one. Adam was created on Friday. It is therefore the image of the crucifixion of the Lord. It is from (Adam) that the peoples propagated. * When (Christ) had prayed on the cross, he said: Let them all be one. Adam was created with the four elements. Christ also united his divinity between the four (elements). Friday is therefore the image of the crucifixion of the Lord. That day, when the blood and water had flowed from the side of the Author of good, the rock

split. The water of life itself entered into the mouth of Adam and became life for him.

9. The entry into heaven and the good thief. - When the spirit of the Lord Jesus had gone out (of his body), he descended into Sheol, with his soul and with (his) divinity. But his body remained on the cross. His divinity was not separated again from the body. By the soul of his body and by his divinity he brought out the elect, and the sent ones, so that they might enter into paradise. He said to them: Go into paradise. Let * the robber, who was at my right, lead you himself. Having heard this, the righteous said: Who is this robber, who was at (his) right? Is it Adam, the father of the world, or Noah, or Abraham, the friend of the Lord, or Isaac, the pure, or Jacob, the saint, or Moses, the meek, or David, who abandoned sin, or Samuel, the upright, or Solomon, the wise, or Josiah, who rendered worship (to the Lord) in righteousness, or Hezekiah, who went into the presence (of the Lord) in justice and in righteousness, or Ezekiel, who saw him between the four fiery animals? Who is this one, who goes into the presence of the Lord and who leaps for joy? Who is the one of whom Our Lord said: Let the robber on the right lead you?
መኑ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘይብል ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ፈያታይ ፡ ዘየማን ፡ ይምራህክሙ ፡፡ ወአውሥአ ፡ ፈያታ”ይ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ኢኮንኩ ፡ አንሰ ፡ እምእለ ፡ ትቤሉ ፡ አን
The robber answered * and said to them: As for me, I am not one of those whom

you yourselves have named. But, I, I washed myself, from my youth, in the blood of men. However, the election beckoned to me. Certainly, from my youth, mercy was placed in my heart. I wept over the heads of the men I had killed. I lamented, and that brought me toward mercy. Having said this, he leaped for joy, after having taken in his hand the book that says: Open the gates, you Seraphim and Cherubim; open for us, open for us. That is how all the righteous said. The Seraphim said to them: (The Lord) will not open for you. The Cherubim also said: He will not let you enter. Immediately this robber, that is to say Dismas, showed the Cherubim the book that was in his hand, and which was written with the blood of the firstborn, * that is to say (with) the blood of Our Lord Jesus Christ, the Only-Begotten Son of the Father. When the angel himself had read this book, he threw the sword of fire from his hand. Then he entered before the robber of the right.

10. Entry of the righteous into heaven. - Following him, all the righteous entered into paradise. Adam, the father of the world, shouted for joy. Noah beat the drum. Abraham gave thanks. Isaac glorified. Jacob praised. Moses clapped his hands. Aaron blessed. Samuel rejoiced. David made the cymbals resound. Solomon admired. Ezekiel and Josiah rejoiced.

Isaiah said: It is (the Lord) Sabaoth. The design that he conceives is a foundation. Job said: He is the patient one who, in his patience, bears the sins of the world. * Jeremiah said: It is to (give) life that the Only-Begotten came. Ezekiel said: We have seen in David, his servant. Daniel said: The vision of the prophets has been sealed. Zechariah said: A light has risen from among men. Amos said: Happiness has been spread over all the earth. Joshua took the drum from the hand of Noah. He sang, saying: The heaven will rejoice and the earth will exult. The whole assembly of the prophets danced, leaped, and shouted, saying: Glory to Him who brought us back into our first inheritance; to Him who, by his death, gratified us with life. It is not (the prophets) only, who rejoiced, but all those who were found in the heavens. Those who (were found) on the earth rejoiced (also). We have mentioned this exposition regarding the work that was created on Friday. Glory to the Creator! Amen.

11. The rest of the Sabbath and the circumcision of the angels. - It is on the first * Sabbath that (the Lord) rested in his Trinity. He ordained by that that one should rest from (all) work. This day is the symbol of the rest of the righteous. It is on this (day) that the angels of sanctification observe the Sabbath. The other angels are sent where the Lord deputes them. The angels of the face and the angels of sanctification have circumcision. As to how the angels

of the face and the angels of sanctification have circumcision, listen to how the Book of Jubilees says: Such is the circumcision of the angels of sanctification. It is therefore just that we all say: Glory to Him who created us for eternal life! Amen. IV.- THE COMPUTATIONS AND THE SYMBOLS (FOURTH TREATISE) 1. Exposition of Abba Batsalota Mikä'êl. - I am going to make another exposition, said Isaac, son of Abba Batsalota Mikä'êl. He wrote computations (on) the sun, * that is to say computations on the dates. It is he who explained how the great ambassadors were born, that is to say the prophets, from Adam until Christ-King, as the book of the Law said: Twenty-two works were done from Adam until (Christ), (that is to say) twenty-two generations. Such are the twenty-two principal generations from Adam until Christ-King. 2. Day of the birth of Adam. - To find the day of the birth of Adam, take one work, then a blessing (and) an epact; add to that the four (constituent) elements of Adam; divide by seven and subtract one; there will therefore remain seven; it is therefore (the day of) the birth of Adam, the seventh of Miyâzyâ.

3. Adam and Jesus. - Adam, the first (man), when he had sinned, went out * of the garden. Our Lord descended (from heaven) in order to save Adam from slavery. Adam went out of his inheritance on Friday. Our Lord also went out for Calvary on Friday, in order to be crucified there. Adam ate (the fruit) at the third hour. Just as Adam and Eve delighted in the tree of the garden, so Our Lord was scourged on his back, to the point that the blood flowed forty times under the lashes. As for the other blows that Our Lord also endured, we cannot count them. When Adam went out of the garden, Our Lord gave him twelve oxen. To Eve also he gave the same thing. The bees went out with her from the garden. The sheep went out with her, five by five, males and females. The goats * went out with her, five by five, males and females. The mules (went out) with her, two by two, males and females. The Lord turned (his) children away from the intensity of their fornication. He also gave (them) (several) of the trees of the garden, that is to say the plantations that Adam had brought out of the garden, (namely): an olive tree, that is to say a mouz; that is why the Law says: The land of the 'elyäs (olive tree), which is the olive tree; 2 pomegranate trees; 3 nards, that is to say nârgê; 4 hazel trees, that is to say laymoun, whose fruits are numerous; 5 mandrakes, that is to say terengue; 6 date palms; almond trees; hazel trees; (sugar plants); vines;

banana trees, that is to say musa ensete. All this, the Seraphim and the Cherubim gave to him, on the order of the living Lord. Adam went out of the garden. * Then the angels, who guarded the tree of the garden, wept with him and accompanied him 12 stadia. That is why it is fitting for man to accompany his neighbor, the stranger. Just as Adam planted the plantations of the garden, so Our Lord also instituted in his coming, 12 apostles and 72 disciples. The asceticism of all the saints is varied. We have mentioned this exposition regarding the creation of Adam.
ወካዕበ ፡ ንዜኑ ፣ ከልአ ፣ ነገረ ፡ በእንተ ፣ ልደቱ ፡ ለሄኖክ ፣ ዘውእቱ ፡ ሳብዕ ፡ እ ምአዳም ፡ ወእምአመ ፡፣ ልደቱ ፣ አቀባ ፡ ለጽድቅ ፣ ወርእየ ፡ ዙሎ ፣ ዘይከውን ፣ ወአ ንከረ ፡፡ ወኣዲ ፡ ርእየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ወወርኅ ፡ ወከዋክብ”ት ፡፡ ወነቢያትሰ ፡ እን ከ ፡ አምሳሊሁ ፡ ለወልድ ፡ ኩሎሙ ።፡፡ ወሄኖክኒ ፡ ጸሐፈ ፡ ኑባሬ ፣ ፲ወ፪ሰንበታተ ፣ ወእግዚአብሔርሂ ፡ አብ ፡ ጸሐፈ ፡ በ ዓጻብዒሁ ፡ ፲ቃላተ ፣ ወይቤ ፡ ሄኖክ ፣ በቀዳሚት ፡ ሰንበት ፡፣ ተወለድኩ ፡ ወእስከ ፣ም ጽአተ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ እትዔገስ ፤ ወዝኒ ፡ ነገር ፡ በእንተ ፣ ወልድ ፡ ውእቱ ።፡ እስመ ፡ በቀዳ ሚት ፡ ሰንበት ፡ ኮነ ፡ ፅረፍት = ወበ ፡፣ ልደቱ ፡፣ ለእግዚእነ ፡ኮነ ፣ እረፍተ ፡ለኩሉ፡፣ ፍጥረት ።፡ ወእስከ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ አእትዔገስ ፡ ዘይቤሂ ፡ ወሄኖክሰ ፡ ይጸንሕ ፡ እስ
We state yet another exposition regarding the birth of Enoch, who is the seventh from Adam. From his birth, he kept justice. He saw everything that would happen and he was astonished. Furthermore, he had visions touching the sun, the moon, and the stars. * The prophets are therefore all the symbol of the Son. 4. The weeks of Enoch. - As for Enoch, he described the institution of the 12 weeks. But the Lord, the Father, wrote with his fingers ten words. Enoch said: The first week, I was begotten, and until the coming of Justice I will wait. This exposition is regarding the Son. Indeed, it is on the first Sabbath that the rest occurred. It is by the birth of Our Lord that the rest occurred for all creatures. Until the

Justice has come I will wait means that Enoch will wait until then. The second week, Enoch saw that evil and cunning had increased. It is in this week that man was saved, he says. Man means Noah, who was saved from the water of the flood. * The second week means the days (of the flood). In the days of Cain and his sons, the habit of evil and cunning increased. Those who glorified themselves vis-à-vis Adam are the (evil) angels. Having clothed themselves in a body, they learned a great sin. These therefore showed all the work they had seen in heaven. The sin, they (learned it because of their pride, because they had clothed themselves in a body. That is why it is not fitting for man to glorify himself. Blessed is he who overcomes Satan, while being clothed in a body. Cain is therefore the father of Enoch. Enoch begat Gâydâd; Gâydâd: Malâl'êl; Malâl'êl: Mâtousâlâ; Mâtousâlâ: Lamech. Lamech is therefore the symbol of Satan. * Lamech killed Cain, that is to say the seventh of his sons. Satan also killed Adam, that is to say the seventh of the seven works, (that) which had been created on Sunday. The Lord punished Cain by the 77 (days) of the flood of rain. He also punished Adam, when (Noah) was carried on the sea with the 77 families (of animals). These are they whom Luke the Evangelist mentioned.
ንጌላዊ ፡፡ ንግባእኬ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ሄኖክ ፣ በሣልስት ፡ ሰንበት ፡ ይቤ ፡ ሄኖክ ፡ ይኅረ
Let us return therefore to what Enoch said. The third week, said Enoch,

(the Lord decided to) choose a man for the implantation of justice, that is to say Abraham. The fourth week, a man was called. An enclosure was made for (the Israelites). A man means Moses, who was called to Horeb. An enclosure was made for (the Israelites) means the tabernacle of Israel, which was made with art. The fifth week, a vision was seen, * that is to say the era of the prophets. The sixth week, said (Enoch), a man ascended. The families of the chosen stock were dispersed. A man means Christ, who ascended the cross. The stock, which was dispersed, means the house of Israel, which was dispersed by the crucifixion (of Christ). The seventh week, said (Enoch), the double of the spirit of the doctrine was given. It is the era of those who will believe in the coming of the Lord. The double of the doctrine means the Old and the New Testament. This generation will become perverse. (The Lord) spoke thus because of the heresy of Rome. The Quatrinitarians are those who call God (the humanity of Our Lord), while the Lord became man. For us, we say: While he was God, Our Lord became * man, and his divinity was not separated from his humanity, for a single hour, nor even an instant. Arius denied the Incarnation of the Son, when he said: God was not begotten of the woman. Others also


[p. 433] {#po.6.p433}
አምላክ ፣ ኢተወልደ ፡ እምብእሲት ፡ ወካልኣንሂ ፣ አምጽኡ ፡ ፅልወታተ ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ ዘዘ ቪአሁ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ማሕተመ ፡፣ ሲኦል ፡
brought many and diverse heresies and were marked with the seal of Sheol.

The eighth week, said (Enoch), a sword will be given. This week is the era of the 318 orthodox. A sword means the Word of the Lord. The eighth week, said (Enoch), will be built the house of the great kingdom, that is to say the churches that were built in the era of King Constantine. The ninth week means the era of the heretics. * The friends and the names of these (are known). The tenth week means the era of the false Messiah, that is to say the end of the world. Then everything will be consumed as says the prophet Daniel. Then it will be the end of the world. Enoch saw all these (things), before they happened.
ሕልቀቱ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ ወዘንተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ሄኖክ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ይኩን ፡፡ ሄኖክ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ተወልደ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትርከብ ፡ ንሣእ ፡ 8አስማተ ፡ ወርኅ ፡ ወ ኒመሳክዊሃ ፡ ለሰማይ ፡ ወ፪አስማቲ፣ሃ ፡ ለፀሐይ ፡ ስመ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ወስመ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ወግ ድፍ ፡ ወፄ፳ጊዜ ፣ ወይተርፍ ፡ ፪ፍጹመ ፣ ውእቱኬ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ልደቱ ፡ ለሄኖክ ፡ አመ፣ጊ ለኅዳር ፡=፡
5. Day of the birth of some prophets. - Enoch. - To find how the prophet Enoch was born, take the four names of the moon and the seven planets of the sky, the two names of the sun, his own name, and the name of his son. Subtract six once. There will remain exactly seven. It is therefore the day of the birth of Enoch, the seventh of Hedâr.
ወከመ ፡ ይትረከብ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ልደቱ ፡ ለኖሕ ፡ ንሣእ ፡ እንከ ፡ ስሞ ፡ ወስመ ፡ አሙ፣ ወስመ ፡ ፻ደቂቁ ፡ ስመ ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡ ወስመ ፡ አንስትያ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ወበረከት ፣ አ”ሜሃ፣
6. Noah. - So that the day of the birth of Noah may be found, take therefore his name, the name of his mother, the name of his three children, the name of his wife, the name of the wives of his children, a blessing, * then two


[p. 434] {#po.6.p434}
፪ወአበቅቴ ፣ ወ፪ግድፍ ፣ ጻወይተርፍ ፡ እንከ ፡ ፪ውእቱኬ ፡ ልደቱ ፡ ለኖኅ ፣ አመ፡ኒ ለሚያዝያ ።
epacts. Subtract two. It will therefore remain seven. Seven is therefore (the day of) the birth of Noah, the seventh of Miyâzyâ.
ወበዕለተ ፡ ተወልደ ፡ ኖኅ ፡ ነበበ ፣ ስብሐተ ፡፣ እንዘ ፣ ይኤምር ፡ ዘደኃሪ፤' ፡ ኩነተ ፡ ዘሰብሑ ፡ ሕፃናት ፡ በኡደተ ፡፣ ሆሳዕና ፡ ኖሕ ፣ እንከ ፣ አምሳሊሁ ፡ ለወልድ =፡ ስማዕ ኬ ፡ እንግርከ ፡ ኖሕሰ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ታቦተ ፡ አምሳሊሃ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወቆሙ ፡ ፳፻ ዘይቤሂ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ ፳፻ቢጽ ።፡ ወግድሙ ፡ ፻፻በአመት ፡ ዘይቤሂ ፣ አምሳለ ፡ የ፻፲ወቿርቱ ዓነ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፍ፡ ወፀስሩ ፡ ዘይቤሂ ፡ አምሳለ ፣ ቋፍሬሆሙ ፡ ለመሃይምናን ፤ ሕጽበተ ፡ ማየ ፣ አይኅ ፡ ዘአንፈሰ ፣ መዓቶ ፣ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ አምሳለ ፣ ጥምቀት ፡ ዘአንፈሰት ፡ ነፍሰ ፡ እምአምልኮ ፡ ጣኦት ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡፣ ዘአምነ ፡ በአብ ፡ ”ወወል ድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አንፈሰ ፡ እምአምልኮ ፡ ጣዖት ፡=
7. Noah, symbol of the Son. - The day he was born, Noah expressed praises (to the Lord), by showing the later scene, in which the children praised (the Lord), at the procession of the Hosanna. Noah is therefore the symbol of the Son. Listen therefore as I explain to you. Noah made the ark, symbol of the Church. Five hundred (animals) stood there. That means the symbol of the five hundred companions. The width (of the ark) was three hundred cubits. That means the symbol of the three hundred and eighteen orthodox. The thirty (cubits) of height mean the symbol of the thirty fruits of the faithful. The washing by the water of the flood, which calmed the anger of the Lord, is the symbol of baptism, which detached souls from the worship of idols, as the apostles said: He who believes in the Father, * in the Son, and in the Holy Spirit, (detaches himself) from the worship of idols.
ወ8በግዓ ፡ ዘሐረደ ፡ ኖሕ ፡ አምሳሊሁ ፡ ለወልድ ፡ ውእቱ ።፡ ወኖሕኒ ፡ ዘሰትየ ፡ ወ ይነ ፤ ወኖመ ፣ ወአምሳለ ፡ ንዋሙ ፡ ለወልድ ፡ በጊዜ ፡ ሕማሙ ። ወክም ፡ ዘሰሐቀ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ እርቃኑ ፡ ለኖኅ ፣ አምሳለ ፡፤ ማሕበረ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ እለ ፡ ሰሐቁ ፡ ላዕለ ፣ ኢየሱ ስ ፡=፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ወንጌላዊ ፡ ወተሳለቁ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡፣ ወሰሐቅዎ ።፡ ወሴም ፣ ወያፌት፣0፣
The unique ewe that Noah slaughtered is the symbol of the Son. Noah, who drank wine and slept, is the symbol of the sleep of the Son, at the time of His Passion. Ham, who laughed at Noah's nakedness, is the symbol of the society of the Jews, who laughed at Jesus, as the Evangelist says: They mocked Him and they laughed at Him. Shem and Japheth, who took their father's garment and covered his nakedness, are the symbol of Joseph and Nicodemus, who
