buried Our Lord. Noah's awakening is the symbol of the resurrection of Christ the King. Qou'a, who devoured his office, is the symbol of Judas, who devoured * his charge, (he) the thief of the purse, the seller of his Lord. O Judas, why did you steal? Does it seem to you that you have gained? Have you not lost yourself? The first dove that Noah sent out is the symbol of the Law, which was sent to Sinai. (The dove) did not find where to rest means the people of the Law, who did not find rest in the first Law, as Zechariah said: He who enters and he who goes out, does not rest from (his) sufferings. This means that he who dies and he who is born, does not rest from the sufferings of Sheol. (There is) also a second dove that was sent by Noah. This one expresses the symbol of the Gospel. The olive leaf that was in the dove's beak is the symbol of the Incarnation of Our Lord, which is described * in the Gospel. (Our Lord is) son of David, son of Abraham, says (the Gospel). Such is the exposition of Noah, (the exposition of the manner) in which he became the image of the Only-Begotten.
ለኖኅ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ አርአያሁ ፡ ለወልድ ፡ዋሕድ ፡፡ ሣልስ ፡ ነገር ፡ በእንተ ፣ ዘከመ ፡ ተወልደ ፡ አብርሃም ።፡ ወከመ ፡ ትርከብ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ልደቶ ፡ ለአብርሃ[ም ፡ ንሣእ ፡ ለከ ፡ ጐልቄ' ፡ ፳ወ8ጳአበው ፡ ወስመ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወስመ፣
8. Birth of Abraham. - The third exposition treats how Abraham was born. To find the day of Abraham's birth, take the number of the twenty-one Fathers, the name of his father, the name of his mother. The twenty-one (Fathers) are, we say, the symbol of Abraham. (Take) a blessing,


[p. 436] {#po.6.p436}
እሙ ፡ ወ፳ወ8ሰ ፡ ዘንቤ ፡ ምሳሌሁ ፡ ለአብርሃም ፤ ወበረከት ፡ አሜሃ ፡ ፳አበቅቴ ፣ ወ፻ግ ድፍ ፡ እንከ ፡ በበሂወይተርፍ ፡ ሂውእቱኬ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ልደቱ ፡ ለአብርሃም ፡ አመ ፡ ለጥ ር ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ግዝረቱ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፤
then an epact. Subtract three (and divide) by seven. Seven will remain. This is therefore the day of Abraham's birth, the seventh of Ter, the day of the circumcision of Our Lord.
ወአብርሃምሰ ፣ ፍጹም ፡ በዙሉ ፣ ወእግዚአብሔርኒ ፡ ፈተኖ ፡ በ፲መከራ ።፡ ወተረክ በ፣ምአመነ ፡ በኩሉ =፡ ወበከመ ፣ ተመከረ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ በ፲መከራ ፡= ወከማሁ ፡ ኮነ፣ አርአያ ፡ ላፅለ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ አ"”ብርሃም ፣፤ ተመከረ ፡ በቀዳሚ ፡ በጸአተ ፡ ምድሩ ፤ ወእ ግዚእነሂ ፡ ተመከረ ፡ በጸአቱ ፣ እምድረ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ወበሑረቱ ፣፤ ውስተ ፡ ምድረ ፡ግ ብጽ ። አብርሃም ፡ ተመከረ ፡ በአባረ ፡ ምድር ።፡ ወእግዚእነሂ ፡ ተመከረ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ ዲ ያብሎስ ፡ አመ ፡ ወጽአ ፡ ለጸዊም ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡=፡ ወአመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ካዕበ ፡ አልቦኑ፣ ሕብስት ፡ ዘናበልኦሙ ፡ ለሕዝብ ፤ አብርሃም ፡ ተመከረ ፡ በመቃብረ ፡ ሳራ ። ወእግዚእ ነሂ ፡ ተመከረ ፡ በሐጢአ ፡ ዘይቀብሮ ፡ እስከ ፡ ሶበ ፣ መጽኡ ፡ ዮሴፍ ፡ ወኒቆዲሞስ =፡ ወገነዝዎ ፡ ወቀበርዎ =፡
9. Abraham, symbol of Jesus. - Abraham was perfect in everything. The Lord tested him with ten temptations. He was found faithful in everything. Just as Abraham was tested by ten temptations, so he became the image of Our Lord. * Abraham was tested in the first place by the departure from his land. Our Lord also was tested by his departure from the land of Israel, and by his coming into the land of Egypt. Abraham was tested by the barrenness of his land. Our Lord also was tested by the devil, when he had gone to fast in the desert, and, furthermore, when he said: Is there no bread, that (we might) give the people to eat? Abraham was tested on (the occasion) of Sarah's tomb. Our Lord also was tested by the lack of people to bury him, until Joseph and Nicodemus had come, (and until) they had shrouded him and buried him.
አብርሃም ፡ ተመከረ ፡ በቀትለ ፡ ነገሥት ፡ አለ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ።፡ ወእግዚአነሂ ፡ ተመከረ ፡ በቀትለ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ አለ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ ላፅሌሁ ።፡፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅትልዎ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ዓርብ ፡= አብርሃም ፡ ”ሞኦሙ ፡ ለሕዝብ ፡ በ፻፻፲ወቿዕደው ። ወእግዚእነሂ ፣ ዴገኖሙ ፡
Abraham was tested by the slaughter of the kings, who had gathered against him. Our Lord also was tested by the slaughter of the Jews, who gathered against him, in order to kill him, on Friday. Abraham * defeated the nations with 318 men. Our Lord also pursued the nations and defeated


[p. 437] {#po.6.p437}
ለአሕዛብ ፡ ወሞኦሙ ፡ ለሰይጣናት ፡ በስብከተ ፡ ፻፻፲ወቿርቱዓነ ፡ ሃይማኖት ።፡ አብርሃም ፡ ተመከረ ፡ በስደተ ፡ ይስማዔል ፡ ወልዱ ፡፡ ዘምስለ ፡ አጋር ፡ እሙ ፡፡ ወእግዚእነሂ ፡ ተመ ከረ ፡ በሞተ ፣ ዓልዓዛር ፡ አርኩ ፣ እንዘ ፣ ቀታሊ ፡ ወማሕየዊ ፡ ውእቱ ፡=፡ አብርሃም ፡ ተ መከረ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ስድዳ ፡ ለዓመት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ወልዳ ፡=፡ ወእግዚእነሂ ፡ ኮኖ ፡ ዕፁበ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ተሰደት ፣ ምዙራብ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ውሉዳ =፡ ምዙራብሰ ፡ ተሰደት ፣ ወተስዕረት ፣ በእ ንተ ፡ ዘሰቀልዎ ፡ ሕዝባ ።፡፡ አብርሃም ፡ ተመከረ ፡ በግዝረት ። ወእግዚእነሂ ፡ ተመከረ ፡ በርግዘተ ፡ ገቦሁ ፤ በኩናት ።፡፡ ግዝረተ ፣ አብርሃም ፡ ወጸጋ ፡ ኮነ ፣ ለአሕዛብ ። "ወርግ ዘተ ፡ ገቦሁኒ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ሕይወተ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ለአሕዛብ ።
the demons through the preaching of the 318 orthodox. Abraham was tested by the expulsion of Ishmael, his son, with Hagar, his mother. Our Lord also was tested by the death of Lazarus, his friend, although he himself was the killer and the vivifier. Abraham was tested, when (the angel) said to him: Cast out the servant with her son. It was hard also for Our Lord, when the temple was overturned with its Levites. The temple was overturned and was destroyed, because the people had crucified (Christ). Abraham was tested by circumcision. Our Lord also was tested by the piercing of his side with the lance. The circumcision of Abraham became grace for the nations. * The piercing of the side of Our Lord became life for the nations.
አብርሃም ፡ ተመከረ ፡ በደፊነ ፡ ዓዘቃቲሁ ። ወእግዚእነሂ ፡ ተመከረ ፡ በደፊነ ፡ት ምህርቱ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ አይሁድ ፡ መሪራን ፡ አለ ፡ ፈቀዱ ፡ ይድፍኑ ፡ ትንሣኤሁ ፣ ቅድስ ተ ፡=፡ አብርሃም ፡ ተመከረ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ በሂደተ ፡ ሳራ ፡ ብእሲቱ ።፡ ወእግዚእነሂ ፡ ተመከረ ፡ በሂደተ ፡ ከላሜዳ ፡ ዘለይ ፡ ዘላፅሌሁ ፡ ወበሂደተ ፡ ልብሰ ፡ እልታሕ ፡ ዘላዕሌሁ ። ወዝ ንቱ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ አርአያሁ ፣ ለእግዚእነ ፣ ዝንቱ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ነገራቲሁ ፡ ለአብርሃም ፡፡ አሚን ፡ ተረክበ ፡ በኅበ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ወአስተርአየ ፡ ጥዩቀ ። ወጥምቀትሂ ፡ ተጠምቀ ፡ አብርሃም ። በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚእነ ፣ በመጽሐፈ ፡ ኪዳን ። ወሀብክዎ ፡ ለአብርሃም ፡
Abraham was tested by the plugging of his wells. Our Lord also was tested by the obstruction of his doctrine on the part of the embittered Jews, who wanted to hide his holy resurrection. Abraham was tested again by the abduction of Sarah, his wife. Our Lord also was tested by the abduction of his purple chlamys, which was upon him, and by the abduction of his tunic, which was upon him. All this, which took place, is the image of Our Lord. Such is the whole exposition of Abraham. Faith was found in Abraham and appeared clearly. Abraham also was certainly baptized, as Our Lord said in the book of the Kidan: I gave Abraham the baptism


[p. 438] {#po.6.p438}
ጥምቀ”ተ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ እደ ፡ የማን ፡ ወዘንተኒ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ዘከርነ ፡ ሕዳጠ ፡ በበይነ ፡ ነገሩ ፡ ለአብርሃም ፡=፡ ወበርሃ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ብርሃኑ ፣ መጠነ ፡ ፲ወ፪ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡፡ ወባሕቱ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ የዓብዩ ፤ እምቅዱሳን ፡ ነቢያት ፣ ወአኮ ፡ ዘየዓብዩ ፣ መነኮሳ ት ፡ በስመ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ አላ ፡ እለ ፡ የዓብዩሰ ፡ እለ ፡ ፍጹማን ፡ በትዕግስት አለ ፡ ኮኑ ፣ ጳውሊ ፡ ወከመ ፡ እንጦኒ ፡ ወመቃሪ ፡=፡
* of life with the right hand. We have mentioned this word, in a few words, regarding the history of Abraham. His light shone and became also (as bright) as (that of) twelve suns. 10. Monks and prophets. - However, monks are greater than the holy prophets. It is not that monks are great by the name of their monasticism. But those who are great are those who are perfect in patience, those who have become (like) Paul and like Anthony and Macarius.
እስመ፤' ፡ እንጦኒሰ ፡ ተመከረ ፡ ፲ወ፪መከራ ፣ ወዝውእቱ ፡ መከራሁ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ በሞ ት ፡ አቡሁ ፡=፡ ወበብእሲት ፣፡ እብራዊት ፡ ወበተመክሮ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ አጋንንት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ከ መ ፡ ላህም ፡ ዘቦ ፡ ቀርን ፡ ወዘቦ ። ክዕበ ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ ሠራዊት ፣ ዘንጉሥ ። በረሐብ ፡ ወበጽምእ ። ወበተገርሞ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ መስቴማ ፡ ከዊ"ኖ ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ አርዌ ፣ ጸዋግ፣ ወበነገረ ፡ ግፅዝ ፣ ይብልዎ ፡ ነምር ፡ ወበውግአተ ፡ ኩናት ፣ ዘአረሚ ።፡ ወበብሂለ ፡ ግባ ፅ ፡ውስተ ፡ ዓለም ፡፡ ወበአንቃል ፤ ዘውእቶ[ሙ ፣፤ ቀማል ። ወበተዓግሶ ፡ ጽፍአት ፡ እምኅበ ፣ መነኮስ። ወበሐለስትዮታት ፡ በተዘርፎ ፣ ገራህቱ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ዘረፍዎ ፡ በአ፳ ፅለት ፡፡ ወበተሰርቆ ፣ ማኅደሩ ፡፡ ወበሕማመ ፡ ርእስሂ ፡፣ ወበሕማመ ፡ ዐይንሂ ፡ እሎን ተ ፣ ፲ወ፪መከራ ፤ ተመከረ ፡ ወበርሃ ፡ ከመ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ፲ወ፪ፀሐይ =፡ ወዐደወ ፡ ብርሃ
11. Saint Anthony. - Indeed, Anthony was tested by twelve temptations. Here are his temptations. First (he was tested) by the death of his father; (then) by a Jewish woman; by temptations on the part of demons, when they (appeared to him) as an ox, which has horns, and also under the appearance of a king's army; by hunger, by thirst; by terrorizations on the part of the persecutor metamorphosing * into the aspect of ferocious beasts; by words of quarrel, it is said; by a tiger; by a spear blow from a pagan; by this word: Return to the world; by vermin, that is to say by lice; by the enduring of slaps on the part of the monks; by wild asses; by the ravaging of his field, which was devastated seven times in one day; by the theft of his dwelling; by headaches, by eye ailments. These are the twelve temptations (by which) he was tested.


[p. 439] {#po.6.p439}
ኑ ፤ እመጠነ ፡ ነቢያት ፣ ወበእንተዝ ፡ ይቤ ፣ 8አምቅዱሳን ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ዘተሰምየ ፣ አር ከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይኔስሕ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፣ ዕበየ ፣ ሐብቶ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፤ ወዝን ቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ነገር ፡ እንበይነ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ክቡር ። ወካዕበ ፡ ዝውእቱ ፡ ነገር ፡ በእንተ ፡ ልደቱ ፡ ለይስሐቅ ፣ ወከመ ፡ ትረክብ ፡ ልደ ቶ ፡ ለይስሐቅ ንሣእ ፡ ለከ ፡ ጐልቁቄ' ፡ ፳ወ፪አበው ፡ ወስሞ ፡ ወስመ ፡ እም ፡ ወአሜ ሃኒ ፡ አበቅቴ ፡ ፳ወበረከተ ፡ 8ወግድፍ ፡ በበ፲፻ጊዜ ፣ ወይተርፍ ፡ እንከ፲ ፡ ውእቱኬ ፤ ፅለተ ፡ ልደቱ ፡ ለይስሐቅ ፣ አመ፤ ፡ ለሳኔ ፡ ወይእቲኬ ፤ አምሳለ ፡ በዓለፃ ፡ ወአምሳለ ፡ በዓለ ፡ ሰዊቶሙ ፡ ለእስራኤል ፡
They shine as much as twelve suns. Their light exceeds the quantity (of that) of the prophets. That is why a saint said of Abraham, who was called the friend of the Lord: He did penance, when he had seen the greatness of the Lord's gift. Such * is the story concerning the illustrious Abraham. 12. Birth of Isaac. - Furthermore, here is the exposition regarding the birth of Isaac. To find the birth of Isaac, take the number of the twenty-two Fathers, his name, the name of his mother, then an epact and a blessing. Divide by seven three times. There will remain ten. This is therefore the day of Isaac's birth, the tenth of Sane. It is therefore the symbol of the feast of Pentecost and the symbol of the feast of the Ears of Corn of Israel.
ወይስሐቅኒ ፤ አምሳሊሁ ፡ ለኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ወንጹሕ ፣ ውእቱ ፣ ወኢያአም ር ፡ ክልኦ ፣ ብእሲተ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ርብቃ ፣ ብእሷቱ ፡=፡ ወበእንተዝ ፣ ተውህቦ ፡ ኑኀ፣ መዋዕል ፡ እምዕለ[ተ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ በዝኀ ፡ መዋፅሊሁ ።፡ ወተ”ሦአ ፡ ሀየንቴሁ ፡ በግዕ ፡ ዝገነት ። ዘመደ ፣ ውእቱ ፡ በግፅ ፣ ዘሦአ ፣ አቤል ፣ ወዘመዱ ፣ ለውእቱ ፡ በግዕ ፡ ዘሦ ኡ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ እምዘመዱ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ በግፅ ፡ ተፈጥሩ ፡ በምድረ ፡ ኤልዳ ፍ=፡ ወበጽ ሐ ፡ክፍል ፡ ለያፌት ፡ ወለካም ። ወአባግዐ ፡ ገነትሰ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ክፍለ ፡ ሴም ፡=፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ዘበጽሑ ፡ ክፍለ ፡ ለሴም ፡ እምእልክቱ ፡ ዐባግኝዓ ፡ ገነት ፡ ስማፅ ፣ እንግርከ ፡ እስመ ፡ ቡሩክ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወቡሩክ ፡ ተወልደ ፡ እምቤተ ፡ ዚአ
13. Isaac, symbol of Jesus. - Isaac is the symbol of Jesus Christ. He was pure. He did not know any other woman than Rebecca, his wife. That is why the length of days was given to him. His days were more numerous than those of his father. * The ewe of the garden was offered in his place. It was of the race of the ewe that Abel had offered, and of the race of the ewe that Israel had offered. (It came) from the race of the ewes (that were created) in the land of Elda. A part (of the ewes) fell to Japheth and to Ham. But the ewes of the garden fell to the part of Shem. Because they fell to the part of Shem, (they are part) of the ewes of the garden. 14. Birth of Jacob. - Listen, that I may explain to you that (Jacob) is blessed


[p. 440] {#po.6.p440}
ሁ። ወበእምቤተ ፡ ያዕቆብኒ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ተወልደ ፡ ስማዕ ፡ እንግርከ ፡ ንሣአ ፡ ለከ ፡ ጐ ልቄ ፡ ፳ወ፪አበው ፡ ስሞ ፡ ወስመ ፣ እሞ ፣፤ ወአሜሃሰ ፣ አበቅቴ ፣ ወ፲ወበረከት ፡ ፪ወግ ድፍ ፡ በበ፪የጊዜ ፣ ወይተርፍ ፡ እንከ ፡ ፲ወ8ውእቱኬ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ልደቱ ፡፣ ለያዕ"ቆብ ፣ አመ ፡ ፲ወ፳ ፡ ለጥር ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ጥምቀቱ ፡ ለእግዚእነ
and that (the Son of the Lord), the Blessed One, was born of his own house, of the very house of Jacob. Listen, that I may explain to you how (Jacob) was born. Take the number of the twenty-two Fathers, his name, the name of his mother, then seven epacts and two blessings. Divide by seven three times. There will remain eleven. This is therefore the day of Jacob's birth, * the eleventh of Ter, the day of the baptism of Our Lord.
ወያዕቆብሰ ፡ ተወልደ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፣ እስመ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡፣ ውእቱ ፡፣ ጸዋሬ ፡ ባሕር ይ ፡ ክቡር ፡ ቡሩክ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እምከርሠ ፡ እሙ ፣ ወኀረዮ ፣ እግዚእነ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ያዕ ቆብሃ ፡ አፍቀርኩ ፡ ወዔሳውሃ ፡ ጸላእኩ ።፡ ወያዕፅቆብሰ ፡ እንከ ፡ አምሳሊሁ ፡ ለወልድ ።= ወዔሳውኒ ፣ አምሳሊሁ ፡ ለሰይጣን ።፡ ያዕቆብ ፣ እንከ ፡ ተባረከ ፡ በኅበ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወዔ ሳውሰ ፣ ኢተባረከ ፡፡ ወለያዕቆብሰ ፡ ተውህሀበቶ ፡ በረከት ፣ እስመ ፡ ሰአሉ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መ ላእክተ ፡ ገጽ ፡ ወመላእክተ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ አንዘ ፣ ይብሉ ፡ ይኩን ፡ በረከት ፡ ላዕለ ፣ ያዕቆ
15. Holiness and role of Jacob. - Jacob was born on the day of sanctification. Indeed, he was holy, he who bore a noble nature. He was blessed from his mother's womb. Our Lord chose him and said to him: I have loved Jacob and I have hated Esau. Jacob is therefore the symbol of the Son. Esau, for his part, is the symbol of Satan. Jacob was therefore blessed by his father. Esau, for his part, was not blessed. It is to Jacob that the blessing was given, for the angels of the face and the angels of sanctification asked for it for him, saying: May the blessing come upon Jacob, for his heart and his intelligence are pure.
ብ እስመ ፡ ንጹሕ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ወሕሊናሁ ፡፡ ወያዕቆብ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ውስት ፡ ናሕስ ፡ መፅአ ፡ ዔሳው ፡ ፈቂዶ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቅት ሎ =፡ ተሓስሶ' ፡ ዘተብሀለ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ መስቀል ፣፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወዔሳውኒ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ ሰይ ጣን ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወ8ማዕዘኒሁ ፡ ለቤት ፡ ዘሀለወ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ያርኢ ፡ ኀበ ፡ 8ወ ንጌላዊያን = ወጸአቶሙኒ ፡ ለደቂቀ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፣ ኀበ ፡ ቀትለ ፡ ዔሳው ፡፣ ያርኢ ፡ ኅበ፣
16. Symbolism of the life of Jacob. - While Jacob was under the roof, Esau came, wanting to kill him. * The quarrel means the symbol of the cross. Esau is the symbol of Satan. The four corners of the house in which Jacob was, indicate the four Evangelists. The departure of the children of Jacob for the killing of Esau indicates the departure of the apostles


[p. 441] {#po.6.p441}
ጸአቶሙ ፡ ለሐዋርያት ፡ ለስብከተ ፡ ወንጌል ፡= ሐፀ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ እንተ ፡ ቀተለቶ ፡ ለዔ ሳው ፡ ትተረጐም ፡ ኅበ ፡ መስቀለ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ እንተ ፡ ቀተለቶ ፡ ለሰይጣን ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ዓርብ ።፡ ወከመ ፡ ወሀበ ፡ አምሀ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ለዔሳው ።፡ ወከማሁ ፡ አግዚእነሂ ፡ ወሀበ ፣ አምሃ ፡ ወጸባሕተ ፡ ለቄሳር ፡ ላባ ፡ ይተረጐም' ፡ በሰይጣን ፣ ወያዕቆብኒ ፡ ይተረጐም ፡ በእግዚእነ ። ወበግዕኒ ፡ እለ ፡ ቦን ፡ ትእምርተ ፡፣ ለያዕቆብ ፡ ወአለ ፡ አልቦን ፡ትእ"ም ርት ፡ ለላባ ፡ እለ ፣ ቦሙ ፣ ትእምርተ ፡ዝይቤ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፣ እሙንቱ ፣ አለ ፡ቦሙ ት እምርተ ፡ ሥላሴ ፡ ወአለ ፡ አልቦሙ ፡ ትእምርት ፡ ዓባግዕፅ ፡ ለላባ ፡ አረሚ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡
for the preaching of the Gospel. The arrow of Jacob, which killed Esau, is explained by the cross of Christ, which killed Satan, on Friday. Just as Jacob gave presents to Esau, so Our Lord also gave presents and tribute to Caesar. Laban is explained by Satan. Jacob, for his part, is explained by Our Lord. The ewes that had the sign were Jacob's. Those that did not have * the sign were Laban's. Those that had the sign mean the Christians, who have the sign of the Trinity. Those that did not have the sign, the ewes of Laban, are the pagans, who do not have faith in the Trinity.
አለ ፡ አልቦሙ ፡ አማነ ፡ ሥላሴ ። ወዮሴፍኒ ፡ አምሳሊሁ ፡ ለወልድ ፡ ውእቱ ።፡ ወካዕበ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትረክብ ፡ ዕለተ ፣ ልደ ቶ ፡ ለዮሴፍ ፡ ንሣእ ፡ ለከ ፡ ጐልቄ፤ ፡ ፲ወ፪አኀዊሁ ፡ ወስመ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወእሙ" ፡ ወስ መ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡=፡ ወአሜሃ ፡ ወአበቅቴ ፣ 8ወበረከት ፡ ፻ወግድፍ ፡ በበ፲፪ጊዜ ፣ ወይተርፍ ፡ እንከ ፡ ኒውእቱኬ ፡ ዕለተ ፣ ልደቱ ፡ ለዮሴፍ ፡ አመ ፡ ኗለነሐሴ ፣
17. Birth of Joseph. - Joseph is the symbol of the Son. Furthermore, to find the day of Joseph's birth, take the number of his twelve brothers, the name of his father and mother, his own name, then an epact and three blessings. Divide by seven two times. There will remain seven. This is therefore the day of Joseph's birth, the seventh of Nahase.
ወበማኅትዊሃ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፣ ተስእሎሙ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ለአርዳኢሁ ፡ ወ ይቤሎሙ ፡ መነ ፡ ይብሉኒ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ዮሴፍ ፡ ተሰይጠ ፡ ለእ፳ብሩ”ር ፡ ወእግዚእነሂ ፡ ተ ሰይጠ ፡ ለ፳ብሩር ፡ ሰ፲ዓመት ፡ ዘጽጋብ ፡ ወኒዓመት ፡ዘረኀብ ፡ እለ ፣ ፈከረ ፡ ዮሴፍ ።
18. Joseph, symbol of Jesus. - On the eve of this day, the Lord Jesus questioned his apostles and said to them: What do men say of me? Joseph was sold for twenty pieces of silver. * Our Lord also was sold


[p. 442] {#po.6.p442}
አዝማነ ፡ ረኀብሰ ፡ እንከ ፡ ያርኢ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አዝማነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ምጽአተ ፡ እግዚእነ ፤ ወጽጋብኒ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ መጽአ ፡ እግዚእነ ።፡ እስመ ፡ መብ ልአ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ወመስቴ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ኃጥእ ፡ ወአሕዛብ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ምጽአተ ፣ እግዚ እነ ፡፡ ዮሴፍ ፡ ኮነ ፣ መጋቤ ፡ በግብጽ ፣ ወእግዚእነሂ ፣ ኮነ ፣ መጋቤ ፣ ሐዳስ ፣ ሥር አት'፣
for thirty pieces of silver. The seven years of abundance and the seven years of famine are those that Joseph explained. The time of the famine therefore indicates the time of the days before the coming of Our Lord. The abundance (indicates) the days after Our Lord had come. Indeed, the sinners and the nations, before the coming of Our Lord, (did not have for) food justice, (nor for) drink justice. Joseph became the dispenser in Egypt. Our Lord also became the dispenser of the New
ወዮሴፍ፡ ነበረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፣ ሞቅሕ ፡ ይዓመተ ፡ ወእግዚእነሂ ፡ ነበረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ግብጽ ፡ ይዓመተ ፡ ወሂአውርጎ ፡ አመ ፡ ጐየ ፡ እምገጸ ፡ ሄሮድስ ፡ ወካዕበ ፡ አ ርአየ ፣ ተሞቅሖ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሰብክ ፡ በቃለ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ፻ዓመተ ፣ ወዮሴፍኒ ፡ ይቤሉ ፡ አ ኀዊሁ ፤ በእንቲ"አሁ ፡ አርዌ ፡ እኩይ ፡ በልዖ ፡ ወአማንኬ ፡ አሉሰ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይፈቅዱ ፡ የሐስዉ ፡ ሀልወ ፡ ተናገሩ ፡ ላፅለ ፣ ርአሶሙ ፣ አራዊትኒ ፡ በልፅዎ ፡ ዘይቤሉ ፡ ለሊሆ ሙ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡እአለ ፡ ሜጥዎ ፡ ለእሁሆሙ ። ወበእንተዝ ፣ ይቤሎሙ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ መጥ ምቅ ፡ ለአለ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ጸሐፍት ፡ ወፈሪሳዊያን ፡ ትውልደ ፡ አራዊተ ፣ ምድ ር፣መኑ ፣ አመረክሙ ፡ ወመኑ ፡ ነገረክሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታምስጡ ፡ እመንሱት ፡ ዘይመጽ እ ።፡ ናሁኬ ፡ ወድአ ፡ተሠይመ ፡ ማሕጴ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጉንደ ፡ ፅፀው ፡፡ ማሕፄሰ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡
Testament. Joseph remained in prison (for) three years. Our Lord also remained in the land of Egypt for three years and seven months, when he had fled from the face of Herod. Furthermore, he made the imprisonment (of Joseph) seen, when he preached the word of the Gospel (for) three years. As for Joseph, his brothers said concerning him: “A wicked beast has eaten him.” Truly therefore these, while they wanted to lie, told the truth about themselves. The beasts have eaten him means themselves, who sold their brother. That is why John the Baptist said to the scribes and the Pharisees, who were coming to him: Generation of vipers, who has shown you, who has explained to you how to escape the coming trial? Behold therefore: already the axe is laid to the trunk of the trees. The axe means the Word of the Lord. The trunk of the
ቃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ውእቱ ።፡ ወጉንደ ፡ ዕፀውኒ ፡ ዘይቤ ፣ ነገሥተ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ እሙ ንቱ ።፡ ወዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ዘከርነ ፡ እንበይነ ፡ ግብሩ ፡ ለዮሴፍ ።፡ ወዝንቱ ፡ ነገሩ ፡ ለዮሴ ፍ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ አርአያሁ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ አሜን ፡ ወአሜን ፡
trees means the kings of the nations. We have mentioned this exposition concerning the history of Joseph. Such is the history of Joseph, who became the image of Our Lord. Glory to Him! Amen. Amen.

19. The testament of the Lord. - The ewe. - We are going to explain further regarding the Testament that the Lord established for the children * of Israel, while they were in the land of Egypt. He said to them: Let a man take a ewe, on the tenth day of the first month, and let it be kept until the fourteenth day. Slaughter it on the fourteenth day, at the moment of the approach of the twilight of the thirteenth day, which shines for the fourteenth day and which shines for the fifteenth. Let it not be eaten in the morning, and let not that which will remain (of it) be eaten. Here is the explanation of this. The head of the ewe means the Word of the divinity, Our Lord Jesus Christ. The hair of the ewe are the gifts of the Holy Spirit, which warm the faithful. Its right (front) legs are the twelve apostles. Its left (front) legs are the prophets Abraham, Isaac and Jacob. Its right (back) legs are the 72 disciples, the 318 orthodox and their 500 colleagues. Its * left (back) legs are those who (come) after them: the 500 (bishops) who were at Constantinople, and the 200 bishops who were at Ephesus. Its ears are the prophets. Its eyes are the patriarchs, the bishops,

the priests, the deacons, the subdeacons and the readers. When a man exposes his sins to the priest, so that they may be forgiven him, while (the man exposes them to him), the priest puts on the cross, and by the inspiration of the Holy Spirit imposes a penance upon him. A great feast occurs concerning this in the heavens. The two horns of the ewe indicate the two kingdoms of the righteous. Their first kingdom will be the Holy Church. Their second kingdom will be on the mountain of Zion and in the new world. He who * will not have entered into the Church, will not be able to enter into the mountain of Zion and into the new world. The back of the ewe, these are the righteous who will receive martyrdom in the days of the false Messiah. Its viscera are the words of the Holy Scriptures, which recount the intimate (thoughts) of the Lord. The breath of its two nostrils is the heat of the faith of the holy martyrs. The ewe is the symbol of Jesus Christ. Glory to Him! Amen.

21. Symbolism of the tabernacle. - Here is the explanation of the ten curtains. Their dimension is the Church. The dimension of a curtain is equivalent to the same thing. The explanation of the tabernacle indicates the souls of the righteous. The ten curtains indicate the ten combats of the saints. The clasps, which are in series of 50, mean the water of the Church. The water of intelligence is the Holy Spirit, which descended on the fiftieth day after the resurrection of Our Lord. The five curtains, which are on the roof, mean the two eyes, the two ears and the mouth. That makes five. Such is the explanation of (these things). The 5 (curtains), which * are connected (and) chained, (and) all the columns, which are erected, mean the symbol of the 5 (books) of the Law of Moses, foundation of the Scriptures. The two bases mean the pedestal of the column, symbol of the prison. They also mean the flesh and the blood of the Only-Begotten. The network means the curtain. The hair fabrics mean the 4 sides of the (tabernacle). The Holy of Holies means the house of Israel, the place of forgiveness, (reserved) for the priest. The porticoes mean the vestibules of the tabernacle. The 42 columns on the side of the sea are the symbol of the 42 generations of the Fathers, which Matthew the Evangelist mentioned. The 15 columns

on the side of the north are the symbol of the 15 prophets. The house of the altar means the house of the (sacred) ministry. The 4 horns of the altar mean the symbol of the * 4 Gospels. The fork means the instrument for skinning the flesh. The censer for the fire means the iron vessels, that is to say the braziers. The hearth on the side of the sunset is (the place) where the fire spends the night. Its length is 100 (cubits). Its crossbars are of bronze; its rings and its palms are of gold. (There are) 14 columns for the first side of the hearth. (There is) the oil of the lamp. The house, where the fire spends the night, is the symbol of Our Lady Mary, in whom the fire of life dwelt. The bronze crossbars mean the bronze horns, that is to say the image of the holy cross. The gold rings mean the symbol of the priests. The 10 columns mean the symbol of the 10 words. The oil of the lamp * means the symbol of the faith, which illuminates the whole world. 22. The priestly garments of Aaron. - The five garments of Aaron are the tunic, which he wore on the Sabbath; the drawers, which he wore in the days of the time of the Testament; the tiara, which he wore in the days of Remembrance; the age of black silk, which the priests wore, when they swept; the logyon, (which Aaron) wore in the days of the seven feasts. In place of the five garments of Aaron, Our Lord also wore (five garments), which are: love, humility, mercy, forgiveness of sin and obedience. The

serpents that were fashioned on the garments of Aaron, in pure gold, are the symbol of the nations, which propagated from the gold of the faith. The drawers of the thighs, that is to say the breeches, are * the symbol of the belt of the monks. The mitre of Aaron is the symbol of the hood (of the monks). The helmet of the head was of gold, of silver and of iron. The hood of the monks is also the image of the crown of thorns with which they crowned Our Lord.
ወ፲ወ፪አዕናቀ፦ ፡ ግሉፋን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጾታ ፡ 8አዕባን ፡ አስማቲሆሙ ፡ ጽሑፍ ፡ለው ሉደ ፣ እስራኤል ።፡ ወ፪አዕባን ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ 8ወንጌል ። ወ፲ወ፪ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ ያዕቆ ብ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ ፲ወ፪ሐዋርያት ። መረግድ ፡ ዘይሁዳ ፡=፡ ወእምነገደ ፡ ይሁዳ ፡ሠረፀ ፡ ዮሐ ንስ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ እአጉሁ ።፡፡ ወበከመ ፡ መረግድ ፣ፃዓዳ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ንጽሐ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለዮሐንስ ፡ ጻዓዳ ፡=፡ ወሰርድዮንሂ ፡ ሕብረ ፡ ቀይሕ ፡ ዘይሳኮር ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ወእምነገደ ፡ ይሳ ክር ፡ ወፅአ ፡ ማቴዎስ ፡ ወንጌላዊ ፡ ጸሓፌ ፡ ትስብእቱ፣ ለወልድ ፡፡ እስመ ፡ሱባኤ ፣ ቀይ ሐ ፡ ነሥአ ፣ እምእግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ።፡ ወጳዝዮንሂ ፣ አምሳለ ፡ መስቀል ።፡ ዘዘብሎን፣ ውእቱ ፡፡ ወጽአ ፡ ፊልፅስ ፡ ወስምዐ ፡ ፅረፍቱሂ ፡ ኮነ ፡ በዲበ ፡ መስቀል ። ወጊሩቱሂ ፡ አብጽሖ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ፈጣሪሁ ፡ ወወሀበ ፡፣ ፍሬሁ ።= ወለኢያስጴድሰ ፡ ቦኅርወተ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ዘሮ ቤል ፡፡ ወእአምቤተ ፡ ሮቤል ፡ወፅአ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ምስለ ፡ እንድርያስ ፡ አጉሁ ፡=፡ ወያስጐረ
23. The precious stones; their symbolism. - The twelve carved gems (are found) in rows of four stones, (on which) are written the names of the children of Israel. The four stones mean the symbol of the four Gospels. The twelve children of Jacob are the symbol of the twelve apostles. The emerald is (the stone) of Judah. It is from the tribe of Judah that John came forth with James, his brother. Just as the emerald is white, so the purity of the flesh of John was white. The sardonyx, with the red color, is (the stone) of Issachar. It is from the tribe of Issachar * that Matthew the Evangelist came forth, the writer of the Incarnation of the Son. Indeed, (Jesus) took the human (nature), (whose blood is) red, from Our Lady Mary. The topaz is the symbol of the cross. It is (the stone) of Zebulun, (from whom) came forth Philip. His martyrdom (and) his death took place on the cross. His goodness made him arrive towards his Creator, (who gave him) his reward. The jasper has three carvings. It is (the stone) of Reuben. It is from the house of Reuben that came forth

Peter with Andrew, his brother. The jasper has three holes; this means the three denials of Peter. (The zeal) of Simeon is like the ember. The zeal of the house of Simeon is like the ember means the scribes and the Pharisees, who were zealous towards the Creator, that is to say the Lord. The sapphire, (it is the gem) which sparkles in an instant. It is (the stone) of Gad. * It is from the tribe of Gad that came forth James, (son) of Alphaeus. Just as the sapphire sparkles in an instant, so also James consumed his martyrdom in an instant. The carbuncle is the stone of Joseph. The interpretation of the carbuncle means that (Joseph) made the ears of corn grow in one day. This word is explained by Thaddaeus. The amethyst is (the stone) of Benjamin. The amethyst means the apostle of the nations. This word is explained by Paul.

The agate is (the stone) of Ephraim. Agate means 'the one who completes.' This word is explained by Matthias. The onyx is (the stone) of Naphtali. Onyx means 'bunch of grapes.' This word is explained by Bartholomew. * The chrysolite means 'the stone of gold.' It is the stone of Dan. It is from the tribe of (Dan) that Judas Iscariot came forth. The gold signifies that (Jesus) had made Judas the guardian of the gold purse. This is why the Evangelist said: 'Indeed, he himself kept our purse and the box of the alms purse.' The beryl means 'the lamp.' It is the stone of Asher. These two words are explained

ወዝኒ ፡ ፪ነገር ፡ ይተረጐም ፡ ኀበ ፡ ቶማስ ፣ ወይእቲሰ ፡ ማኅቶት ፡ ዘተብሕለት ፡ እስመ ፡ ከመ ፡ ማኅቶት ፡ አብርሃ ፡ ትምሀርቱ ፡ ለመንታ ።፡፡ ወቶማስ ፡ ብሂል ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ብሂል ፡፡ ወከመዝ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ፍካሬሆን ፡ ለአዕናቀ፦ ፡ አለ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልብሱ ፡ ለአሮን ፡ ካሀን ፡= ወል ብሰ ፡ መትከፍትኒ ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ክሳዱ ፡ ለአሮን ፡ ይተረጐም ፡ በአሕባል ፡ ዘወደዩ ፡ ው ስተ ፡ ክሳዱ ፡ ለእግዚ”እነ ፡ ቀዳማዊ ፡ ይነግር ፡ በእንተ ፡ ደኃራዊ ፡ ወደኃራዊ ፡ ይፌጽ ሞ ፡ ለቀዳማዊ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ለገባሬ ፡ ኩሉ፣ አሜን።
by Thomas. The lamp signifies that the doctrine of Didymus shines like a lamp. Thomas means 'the sun.' Such is the explanation of the precious stones that were in the garments of the priest Aaron. The shoulder garment, which was on Aaron's neck, is explained by the ropes that were put on the neck of Our Lord. * The former (events) explain the latter. The latter consume the former. Glory to the Author of all (things)! Amen.

24. Concerning the priesthood. - The Law speaks about the ordination of Aaron. Such is the explanation (of the Law). The ox, which is consumed outside, is the symbol of Christ, who was killed outside the camp. That his blood be sprinkled on the four corners of the altar means the four patriarchs, who were sprinkled by the blood of Christ. The fat of its belly is explained by the society of the apostles. The two kidneys are explained by Peter and Paul. The other sheep is also interpreted as Christ. The blood, with which the temple is filled, is explained by the blood of Christ, with which the Church is filled. 'It was sacrificed in each of its members' * means the members of Our Lord: the apostles in their order, the martyrs in their order, the monks in their order, the priests in their order, the children in their order. 'You shall wash its intestines in water.' Its intestines mean the children of baptism. You


[p. 450] {#po.6.p450}
ንቱ ፡ ውሎደ ፡ ጥምቀት ፡ ወታነብር ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዘይክል ፡ ዝኒ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለእግ ዚእነ ፡ ዘውስተ ፡ ጻሕል ፡ ዘእድ ፣ ብሂል ፡ ወበግዖሰ ፣ ኩሎ ፡ ታዓርግ ፣ ውስተ ፡ ምሥ ዋዕ ፣ ዘይቤሂ ፣ በእንተ ፡ ዘአርገ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፣ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሀበ ፡ አቡሁ ፣ ወአ ፅረገ ፡ ለአቡሁ ፡ ዕጣነ ፡ ወመሥዋዕተ ፡ ክቡረ ።
shall place its head where it may be. This means the body of Our Lord, which is in the hand-paten. 'All the sheep, you shall offer them to the altar' signifies that Our Lord Jesus Christ ascended to his Father and offered to his Father the incense and the glorious sacrifice.

The perfect sheep is the symbol of the Lord. The blood (of the sheep, with) which it was anointed on the tip of its ear, is the symbol of the blood of Christ, with which * the faithful were anointed. It is by the first anointing, (done) by hand, that the ordination of the priests of the Gospel was accomplished. The breast of the sheep means the thorax, upon which the teats grow. The two perpetual sheep of the morning and the evening are explained by the prayer of the saints, which the angels cause to rise in the evening and in the morning. When the angels make a sound, the cock crows immediately. The five unleavened cakes are interpreted as the five wise virgins.
ወልብሰ ፡ ዘርዜቅ ፡ ኀሪረ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ አምሳለ ፣፤ እልታሕ ፡= ወይእቲ ፡ ልብስ ፡ ፪ኅብ ራ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ 8ኀራ ፡ እለ ፡ ተዐጸው ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ልብሰ ፡ እግዚእ ፡፡ ወግዱም ፡ ዘርዜቅ ፡ ዓ ውዶ ፡ ልብሰ ፡ ሎግዮን ፡ እንተ ፡ ይለብስዋ ፡ ሱራፌል ፡ በበዓላት ፡ ዓበይት ፣ ወ”ጽንጌ ፡ ሮማን ፡ ቀዬሕ ፣ አምሳለ ፣ ክዕወተ ፣ ደሞሙ ፡ ለሰማዕት ፡ ወዘንተ ፣ አርአዮ ፣ ለሙ
The silk jersey, (color) of gold, is the symbol of the tunic. The garment of four colors is the symbol of the four soldiers who cast lots for the garment of the Lord. The jersey is wrapped around the garment of the breastplate that the Seraphim wear on great feasts. * The red pomegranate flower is the symbol of the shedding of the blood of the martyrs. (The Lord) showed this to Moses. Concerning the dedication, he ordered him to bring in and told him to bring in all (the people). The twelve princes of

ሴ ። ወበእንተ ፡ መድቅሕ ፡ አዘዞ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያብእ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያብእ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ወ አብኡ ፡ ፲ወ፪መላእክት ፡ ኀይል ፡ አምሳለ ፣ ፲ወ፪መላእክት ፡ ዘውእቶሙ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ወ ዝውእቱ ፡ ዘአብኡ ፡ ለለ8መላእክተ ፡ ኀ[ይ]ል ፡፡ መጽብሕ ፡ 8ዘብሩር ፡ ዘ፻ወ8ሐሳቡ ፡ ወፍያል ፡ ዘብሩር ፡ 8ዘ፳ሰቅሎን ፡ በሰቅል ፡ ቅዱስ ። መፅብሕ ፡ ብሂል ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ገይበ፣፤ ወርቅ ፡ ፲ወ፪ወርቁ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ሴጡ ፡፤ ብሂል ።፡ ወኅኀሳቡ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ እስመ ፡ ያስተዋፅእዋ ፡ በላዕለ ፣ ፻8ብእሲ = ወድበሰቅሎን ፣ ዘይቤሂ ፡ ውእቱኒ ፣ ያስተዋጽእዎዖ ፡ በላዕለ ፣፡ ድብእ ሲ ፡፡ ወመጽብሕ ፡ ዘይቤኒ ፡ ” ካዕበ ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ አቡ[ነ ፡ አዳም ፡ ዘይቤሎ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ አምሳልየ ፡ ወአርአያየ ። ወፍያልኒ ፡ ዘውእቶሙ ፡ ቱርርባናት ፡=፡ ወበእንተዝ ፡ ይ ብል ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ እምውስተ ፡ ፍያላት ፡ ምጽዋተ ፡ ወሀብከ ፡ ለአዕይንቲከ ፡ ድኅረ ፡ ተ
the army brought it in, a symbol of the twelve princes, that is to say, the apostles. This means that each of the princes of the army brought it in. A tribute of silver of the value of 130 (shekels), and a silver cup of 70 shekels, according to the holy shekel, (are symbols). The tribute means the golden chalice. The twelve (pieces of) gold mean the price (of the chalice). Its value (is 130 shekels) signifies what was paid for 130 men. (Its value is) 70 shekels also means what was paid for 70 men. The tribute means * also the symbol of our father Adam, of whom the Lord said: 'He is my likeness and my image.' The cup means the oblations. This is why Solomon said: 'The pearls, coming from your cup, you have given as alms, then you went naked like a roof.'
ኀውር ፡ ዕራቅከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ናሕስ ፡=፡ ንግባእኬ ፤ ኅበ ፡ ጥንተ ፡፣ ነገረ ፣ ጻሕል ፣ ዘዐሥሩ፤' ፡ ወርቁ ፣ ጻሕልሰ ፣ አምሳሊ ሁ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ጻሕለ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ወዓሥሩ ፡ ወርቁ ፡ ዘይቤሂ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ዓ፲አጻብዓ ፡ አደዊሁ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ አለ ፡ ስፉሓን ፡ በዲበ ፡ ዕፀ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ወምሉዕ ፡ ዕጣን ፣፡ ውስቴ ቱ ፡ ዘይቤሂ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ጸሎተ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡፡ ወበከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ አቡ ፡ ቀለምሲስ ፡ ወሀብ ዎ ፡ ዕጣናተ ፡ ከመ ፡ የሀብ ፡ ለጸሎተ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ቅ”ዱሳን ። ወላህም ፡ ፳ዘይቤሂ ፡ ላ ህመ ፣ መግዝፅሰ ፡ ለሊሁ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፣ ወ8በግዕ ፣ ዘይቤሂ ፡ በግዕኒ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡፡ ወከመ ሰ ፡ በግዕ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ስማዕ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ኢሳይያስ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ይጠባሕ ፡ ከመ ፡ በግዕ ፡=፡ ወ
Let us return then to the beginning of the exposition of the paten of ten (pieces of) gold. The paten is the symbol of Christ, the paten of life. The ten (pieces of) gold mean the ten fingers of the hands of Our Lord, which were stretched out on the wood of the cross. 'The incense is abundant therein' means the prayer of the saints, as Abu Qalamsis said: 'Incense is given (to the Lord), so that he may hear the prayer of all * the saints.' An ox; the fat ox means Christ himself. A sheep; the sheep means Christ. As to how the sheep is Christ, listen to what Isaiah said: 'He came, in order to be slaughtered like a sheep.'


[p. 452] {#po.6.p452}
ማሕስአ ፡ በግዕኒ ፣ 8ዘይቤሂ ፣ ውእቱኒ ፣ አምሳሊሁ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፣ ዘተማሕስአ ፡ በሥ ጋ፣ሰብእ።= ፳ዘይቤሂ ፣ ዘውእቱ ፣ ፳መክሊት ፡ ዘገብር ።፡ ወ፳አጣሊ ፡ ዘውእቶሙ ፡ 5ምስፍና ፡ ' ኢሎፍሊ ፡ ጌት ፡ አስቀሎና ፡ ጋዛ ፡ አቃሮን ፡፡ ወ፳አባግፅ ፣ አንስተ ፡ ዘይቤሂ ፡ አምሳሊ ሆን ፡ ለ፳ደናግል ፡ ጠባባት ። ወ፪አጐልት ፣ ዘይቤሂ ፣ አምሳሊሆሙ ፡ ለአሕዛብ ፣ ኤዶ ም ፡ ወለአሕዛበ ፡ ሞአብ ፡ እለ ፡ ተጸውዑ ፡ ውስተ ፣ አሚን ። ወበአንተ ፡ ሞአብ ፡ይ ብል ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወሞአብ ፡ ካህ”ን ፣ ተስፋየ =፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ኤዶምያስ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ኤይምሰ ፡ አሰፍሕ ፡ መከየድየ ፣ ወመከየድየ ፡ ይብል ፡ በእንተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክር ስቲያን ፡ ቅድስት' ፡ ተሰፍሐት ፡ ለአሕዛብ ። ወካህንኒ ፡ ዘተሰምየ ፣ ወልደ ፡፣ እግዚ አብሔር ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አሕዛብ ። ወኩሉ ፡ መድቅሐ ፣ ደብተራ ፣፡ አምሳሊሁ ፣ ውእ ቱ ፡ ለዘሀሎ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡፡ ወህየንተ ፡ አለ ፡ አብኡ ፣ መድቅሐ ፡ በቀዳሚ ፡ ሕግ ፡ ዝን ቱ ፡ ዘዘከርነ ፡ አምኀሃ ፡ ለቤተ ፣ ክርስቲያን ።፡ ወሀየንተ ፡ መዳምቲሃ ፡ ለደብተራ ፡ ዘይ ቤ ፣ መዝሙረ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡፡ ወመዳምሕኒ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ጸዋልቅ ፡ ብሂል ፡ ወድንባዛቲሃኒ ፡ ዝጓዛቲሃ ፡ ለለታቦት ። ወባዕ ፡ ዝየ ፣ ወዝየ ፡ ዘይቤሂ ፡ ዝየ ፡ ወዝየ ፡ ዕሩይ ፡ ብሂል ፣
A lamb of the sheep; this also means the symbol of Christ, who made himself a lamb through the flesh of man. Five means the five talents of the servant. The five goats mean the five principalities of the Philistines: Gath, Ashkelon, Gaza, Ekron. The five sheep mean the symbol of the five wise virgins. The two heifers mean the symbol of the people of Edom and the people of Moab, who were called to the faith. Concerning Moab, the Holy Spirit said: 'Moab, the priest, is * my hope.' But concerning Edom he said: 'Upon Edom I will extend my footstool.' My footstool means the Holy Church, (which) is extended for the peoples. The priest, who is called the Son of the Lord, is for all peoples. Every dedication of the tabernacle is the symbol of the one who was to come. In place of those who introduced the dedication in the first Law (means) the gifts of the Church, which we have mentioned. In place of the cups of the tabernacle means the psalter of the saints. The cups mean the basins. The bars (mean) the reed mats of each sanctuary. 'Between here and here.' Here and here means equal and is the symbol of the Trinity.
አምሳሊሁ ፡ ለሥላሴ ፡፡ ወማዕጠንት ፡፣ ዘወርቅ ፡ አምሳሊሁ ፡ ለዋህድ ፤ ወዕጣንኒ ፡ ወተ"”'ነ ፣ ጢስ ፡ ዘእም
25. Incense, symbol of the sufferings of Christ. - The golden censer is the symbol of the Only-Begotten. The incense and * the vapor of the smoke that rises from it are


[p. 453] {#po.6.p453}
ውስቴቱ ፡ የዓርግ ፡ አርአያ ፡ ሕማማቲሁ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ዘአዕረገ ፣፡ ለአቡሁ ፡ ዕጣነ ፡ ወ መሥዋፅዕቶ፣፡ክቡረ ።፡ ወበእንተዝ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡፡ በእንተ ፡ ነፍሰ ፡ክርስቶስ ። ወ መኑ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ እንተ ፡ ተዓርግ ፡ እምገዳም ፡ ከመ ፡ ሠርፀ ፡ ጢስ ፡ ዕጥነታ ። እም ገዳም ፡ ይብለከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይብጻሕ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ለሊሁ ፡ ናሁ ፡ አዐርግ ፣ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ወይ ሰቅልቓዎ ፡ ለወልደ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመሕያው ።፡ ወከመ ፡ ጢስ ፡፣ ዘይወፅእ ፡ እመሳክው ፡ ከማ ሁ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ሕማማት ፡ ወፅአ ፡ እምላዕለ ፡ እግዚእ ።፡ ተእኅዘ ፡ ተሰቅለ ፡ ሐመ ፡ ተጸፍ ዓ ፡ ተኩርዐ ፡ ዘባኖ ፡ ተዘብጠ ፡ ወህየንተ ፡ ማይ ፡ ሐሞተ ፡ ሰትየ ፡ ወፍትሐ ፣ሞት፣ ኅቤሁ ፡ ገብአ ፡፡ ወበሕማማቲሁ ፡ መንበረ ፡ አብ ፡ ተአጥነ ፡፡
the image of the sufferings of Christ, who offered to his Father the incense and his glorious sacrifice. This is why Solomon said concerning the soul of Christ: 'Who is this, who comes up from the desert like the smoke of a perfume (that burns) spreads?' 'In order that he come from the desert,' you were told (Solomon), means (Christ) himself, (who said): 'Behold: I will go up to Jerusalem. The Son of man will be crucified.' Like the smoke that comes out of the windows, thus were numerous the sufferings (that) came out of the Lord. He was seized; he was crucified; he suffered; he was slapped; he received blows of the fist; he was struck on his back; in place of water he drank gall; the sentence of death returned to him. By his sufferings the throne of the Father was perfumed.
ወታቦ”ትኒ ፡ እንተ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ኦሪት ፣ አምሳሊሃ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ለእግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያ ም ። ወ፲ቃላት ፡ አለ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ታቦት ፣ አምሳሊሁ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ስብሐተ ፣ ሕይወት ፡ ቃለ ፡ አብ ፡ አማንኬ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ወኃረያ ፡ ለማርያም ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ድን ግል ፡ በሥጋሃ ፡ ድንግል ፣ በሕሊናሃ ፡ ድንግል ፡ በአፉሃ ፡ ወድንግል ፡ በአዕይንቲሃ ።፡ በ ስጋሃ ፡ አልባቲ ፡ ደነሰ ።፡ ወበሕሊናሃ ፡ ኢሐለየት ፡ እኩየ ፡ እስመ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኮ ነ ፤ ማእዖ ፡ ወመንሰገ ፡=፡ ወአፉሃ ፡ ኢነበበ ፡ እኩየ ፣ ወአዕይንቲሃ ፣ ኢተሰግረ ፡ በእኩ ይ ዘንተ ፡ 8ድንግልና ፥ ረከብነ ፡ በኅበ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡=፡ ወሰብእሰ ፡ አልቦ ፡
26. The Virgin Mary. - The ark * in which was the Law is the symbol of Our Lady Mary. The ten words that were in the ark are the symbol of Christ. The Glory of Life, the Word of the Father, has therefore truly become man. He chose Mary Our Lady, virgin in her body, virgin in her intelligence, virgin in her mouth, virgin in her eyes. In her flesh she had no impurity. In her intelligence she did not think evil, for the Holy Spirit became (for her) the latch and the bolt. Her mouth did not speak evil. Her eyes were not ensnared by evil. This unique virginity, we find it in Our Lady Mary. Man, himself, is not perfect. If he does not sin in his flesh, sometimes he sins in his intelligence.


[p. 454] {#po.6.p454}
ፍጹመ ፡ እመ ፡ ኢስሕተ ፡ በሥጋሁ ፡ ቦ ፡ አመ ፡ ይስሕት ፡ በሕሊናሁ ። ወሕሊና ፡ መ ላእ”ክትኒ ፡ ኢይትዔረዮ ፡ ለንጽሐ ፡ ሕሊና ፡ ለእግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ።
The intelligence of the * angels is not equal to the purity of the intelligence of Our Lady Mary.
ወዘንተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ነገር ፡ ዘደብተራ ፡ እንበይነ ፡ ነገሩ ፣ ለሙሴ ፡=፡ ወተወልደ ፡ ከመ፲ ወ፻ለነሐሴ ፤ አልቦ ፡ አበቅቴ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ አምሳሊሁ ፡ ለወልድ ፡፡ ወኢያሱኒ' ፡ ኅበ ፡ ትእግ ሥቱ ፡ ለወልድ ፣ አምሳሊሁ ፡ ለወልድ ።፡ ወኢያሱኒ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ኔዌ ፡ አምሳሊሁ ፡ ለወ ልድ ወተወልደ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አልቦ ፡ አበቅቴ ፣፡ አመ ፡ ወለታኅሣሥ" ፡=፡ ወርኢኬ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ገሀደ ፡ አርአያሁ ፡ ለወልድ በከመ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ተወረሱ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ምድ ረ ፡ ርስት ፍ=፡ ከማሁ ፡ ተዋረሱ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡=፡ ኢያሱ ፡ እ ንከ ፡ ፈነወ ፡ ፪ሰብአ ፡ ዓይን ፡ አምሳለ ፡ ኦሪት ፡ ወነቢያት ፡፡ ወራዓብኒ" ፡ አምሳለ ፡ ቅድ ስት ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡=፡ ወፈትለ ፡ ቀዩ”ሕ ፡ ዘአሰረት ፡ በመስኮተ ፡ ቤታ ፣ አምሳለ ፡ ደም ፡ ዘውሕዘ ፡ እምገቦሁ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ።
27. Joshua, symbol of Jesus. - This whole exposition of the tabernacle is about the history of Moses. (Moses) was born on the 13th of Nahase. There was no epact. He became the symbol of the Son. Joshua by his patience is the symbol of the Son. Joshua, son of Nun, is the symbol of the Son. He was born (at a time) when there was no epact, the ... of Tahshash. See then that he clearly became the image of the Son. Just as, (when) Joshua had entered, Israel inherited the land of the inheritance, so the Christians inherit the Holy Church. Joshua, furthermore, sent two scouts, a symbol of the Law and the prophets. Rahab is the symbol of the Holy Church. The red thread * that she tied to the window of her house is the symbol of the blood that flowed from the side of Our Lord.
ወተሰርዎ ፡ ከናአን ፡ በእደ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡=፡ ተሰርዎ ፡ አጋንንት ፡ በእደ ፡ እግዚእ ። ወበ ከመ ፡ በመዋዕለ ፡ ኢያሱ ፡ ቆመት ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ 8ዕለት ፡ ፪ኮነ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ በዕለተ ፣ ስቅለ ቱ ፡ ለእግዚእ ፡ 8ዕለተ ፡ ሰኑየ ፡ ኮነ ።፡ ኢያሱ ፡ እንከ ፡ ነሥአ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ፍጹመ ኢያሱ ፡ እንከ ፡ ኅረየ ፣ ፲ወ፪አዕባነ ። ወኢየሱስኒ ፡ ኅረየ ፡ ፲ወ፪ሐዋርያተ ፡=፡
The extirpation of Canaan (took place) by Joshua. The extirpation of demons (took place) by the Lord. Just as in the days of Joshua the sun stood still, and one day became two (days), so on the day of the crucifixion of the Lord one day became two days. Joshua therefore took the sign of the Son perfectly. Joshua, moreover, chose twelve stones. Jesus also chose twelve

apostles. Joshua chose seven jubilee trumpets, to sound. The Lord Jesus gave seven clerics to the Church. In the days of Joshua, Achan was found a thief. In the days of the Lord, Judas was found a thief of the purse. In the days of Joshua, * the children of Israel all obeyed (Joshua) with one heart. (In the days) of Jesus Christ, the people of the Christians obeyed (Jesus Christ) with one heart and (in) one faith. In the days of Joshua, the people abandoned murmuring. In the days of the Lord, the people abandoned the worship of idols. Joshua distributed to the Israelites the land (promised, giving) to each (his share). Christ organized the Church and organized the Christians, (putting them) each in his rank and each in his place. In the days of Joshua, moreover, good came to pass. In the days of Jesus, the people (of the Lord) inherited the kingdom of heaven. In the days of Joshua, it happened that they surpassed one another in killing. In the days of the Lord, the holy * apostles surpassed one another in becoming martyrs. Here is what we recount concerning Joshua, son of Nun. He became the shadow and the image of the New Law.

28. David. - Here is the history of David, son of Jesse. To find the birth of David, take the number of the ten strings (of the psaltery), the name of his father and his mother, and the number of fifty psalms. Divide by seven, until all (the calculation) is finished. What will remain for you is therefore (the day

of) the birth of David, the seventh of Miyazya, the day on which Adam was born. David became the image of the Son. According to the flesh, (David) is called the father of Our Lord, because Our Lady Mary came forth from David. He himself prophesied how (the Son) would come, how he would be born, how he would be crucified and would rise again.

29. David and the Trinity. - Concerning the Trinity, he * prophesied profound (things). He said: 'Who is God besides the Lord? Who is the Lord besides our God? It is the Lord, who girded me with strength.' See then that he said 'Lord' three (times), concerning the Trinity. He said again, concerning the Trinity: 'I seek your face; I seek your own face, O Lord. Do not turn your face from me.' Furthermore, he said: 'The Lord said to my Lord: Sit at my right hand.' That is concerning the Trinity. Moreover, he said: 'I will enter the altar of the Lord, toward the God who gladdens my youth. I will entreat you, (O) my God, with the zither.' That is the image of the Trinity. He said again: 'Therefore the Lord, your God, has anointed you with * the oil of joy, in preference to those who are like you.' 'In preference to those who are like you' means in preference to the prophets. 'Your God' means that (Christ) has clothed himself
መ ፡ ሥላሴ ፡ አርአየ ፡ በዝኒ ። ወለሊሁኒ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እግዚእ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያርኢ ፡ ስላሴሁ ፡ ዓ ዓርግ ፡ ሰማየ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አቡየ ፡ ወአቡክሙ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አምላክክሙ ፡፡ ዘንተ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሥላሴ ፡=፡ ሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ለገባሬ ፡ ዙሉ ፣ ፍጥረት ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፣ አሜን ፣ ወአሜን።
with the flesh of man. See then that (David) showed the Trinity by these (words). The Lord himself also said, showing his Trinity: 'I ascend to heaven toward my Father and (toward) your Father, toward your God.' David said that concerning the Trinity. Glory to the Author of every creature for ages of ages! Amen. Amen.
